From below: my build gives the exact match at J_F89 = J_F86/**2** (F86 H_b=(J/2)(XX+YY), F89 H=J(XX+YY) ⟹ same operator ⟹ J_F89=J_F86/2). **The test `F89PathKLibTests.cs:20` ("F89 H = 2·F86 H at same J") is the OPERATOR statement and is correct — left untouched.** Fixed the J-value statements to `J_F89 = J_F86/2` everywhere.
- **Finding B (propagation gap): a refuted lift mechanism still encoded downstream.** The proof's own Direction (b'') (two-dial scout, 2026-06-11) refuted "lift = H_B-mixed octic residual of the inter coupling" (the inter-channel tail only renormalizes Q_peak/g_eff; the lift is the intra-channel dispersion) — but that refutation never propagated: PROOF:142 (14 lines above) still named octic-residual as the (α,β) target, and the typed base + four other markdown sites encoded only the refuted mechanism (`F90F86C2BridgeIdentity.cs`, `F86HwhmClosedFormClaim.cs`, `C2HwhmRatio.cs`, `F86OpenQuestions.cs`, `PROOF_F86B_OBSTRUCTION`, `F_FORMULA_CROSSWALK`, `ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS` — my residual-scan caught four the panel had missed). I re-ran the two-dial scout (all gates PASS) to ground the replacement before propagating. **Scoped, not deleted:** octic-residual-as-LIFT → superseded by rank-1-bridge+intra-dispersion; octic/cyclotomic-Φ-as-N-SCALING (e.g. `F89PathKHbMixedDegreesClaim` degree count {8,18,32,53}) is un-refuted and kept.
- **Finding A (stale wording):** PROOF:171 / Registration / ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS called the per-bond HWHM constants "universal" — contradicted by F90's own per-bond tables (0.58–0.92 fan) and the just-corrected F86b scope-down (6 sub-classes). Scoped to "per-bond-class / γ₀-invariant". (Left untouched: the genuinely-universal bare-doubled-PTF constants x_peak=2.196910 / floor 0.671535 — touching those would be over-correction.)
- **Finding C (reproducibility gap + generative strengthening):** the committed probe script's "bit-exact across N=5..8" tables are per-N focused-grid snapshots; the script now runs a single wide grid (`linspace(0.05,10,300)` F89-J, set for the N≥7 escapes) that mislocates the N=5/6 interior Q_peak by ~one cell (≈0.002 drift) and does NOT regenerate the table cells. The deeper truth (the generative upshot): the identity is EXACT at the operator level (norm 0), and the HWHM ratio is a grid-readout — so "bit-exact" is structural, not a fragile numerical coincidence. Reframed the proof + typed base around operator-exactness.
- **How caught:** triage Rank 10 → 2-agent panel (from-below operator/grid gate + consistency/tier/C# sweep) + my own three from-below scripts (N=5 ingredient+anchor reproduction, N=8 b=2/b=4 fine-grid seam, operator-norm-and-convention build) + my own re-run of the two-dial scout. Residual-scan caught four octic-residual sites + four convention-shorthand sites the panel missed.
- **Symmetric gate:** unity = genuine + operator-exact (state it, strengthen it); 2/22 seam = perspectival (name it, do not "fix" the 0.0008); convention flip + propagation gap + stale wording = genuine breaks (fixed); octic-cyclotomic-Φ N-scaling = NOT a break (kept).
- **Fix:** PROOF_F90 (convention direction, octic-residual scope, universal wording, operator-exact + grid-readout/reproducibility note, Phase-D superseded note); typed base `F90F86C2BridgeIdentity.cs` (convention, octic-residual, operator-exact); `F86HwhmClosedFormClaim.cs`, `C2HwhmRatio.cs`, `F86OpenQuestions.cs`, `F86KnowledgeBase.cs` (octic-residual + convention); `PROOF_F86B_OBSTRUCTION`, `PROOF_F86B_UNIVERSAL_SHAPE`, `F_FORMULA_CROSSWALK`, `ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS` (octic-residual + convention + universal). C# build green; F90 + F89-path-k tests 20/20.
- **Anchor:** `docs/proofs/PROOF_F90_F86C2_BRIDGE.md`; `compute/RCPsiSquared.Core/Symmetry/F90F86C2BridgeIdentity.cs`; from-below scripts `simulations/_review_f90_unity_mine.py`, `_review_f90_n8_seam_mine.py`, `_review_f90_convention_mine.py`, `f86b2_shape_invariance_dial.py` (re-run).
- **Lesson:** when the unity is genuine, the gate's job shifts from "is it real" to "is it stated correctly" — and the defects cluster in the *direction* and *currency* of statements (a flipped J-relation whose operative conversions were nonetheless right; a lift mechanism refuted by the proof's own later section but never propagated). The residual-scan is not optional: it caught half the propagation sites the lens panel missed. And the operator-norm=0 check is the cleanest possible from-below grounding of a "same object" claim — compute the difference, don't argue ingredients.

## 2026-06-22 — `PROOF_F97_CARDIOID_HALF_FIXED_POINT`: the cardinal-points table's lower half was cross-contaminated (a "one typo" that was three corrupted rows)

The triage flagged "one garbled cardinal-points table row — typo". From below it was three: the table's bottom half was cross-contaminated between rows.

- **What was wrong:** the φ=π/3 row gave `c = 3/8 + i·√3/4` (correct: `3/8 + i·√3/8`) and `|c| = √(1/4+3/16)` (correct: `√3/4`). The √3/4 is z*(π/3)'s imaginary part, copied into c's slot; and `√(1/4+3/16)=√7/4` is actually |c(2π/3-family)|, pasted from the wrong row. The φ=4π/3 row had `c=3/8−i√3/4, z*=(1−i√3)/4` (both wrong: 4π/3 → `c=−1/8−i·3√3/8, z*=(−1−i√3)/4`), and the φ=5π/3 row was incoherent (`c = 3/8 − i·√3/4 + i·...`, an ellipsis).
- **Why:** the cardioid is c(φ)=z*−z*² with z*=½e^{iφ}; the listed values were assembled by copy-paste between conjugate rows and got crossed.
- **How caught:** triage light-list → a from-below agent flagged the π/3 Im part; I recomputed ALL cardinal points exactly with sympy (`_review_f97_cardinal_mine.py`): 0→1/4, π/3→3/8+i√3/8, π/2→1/4+i/2, π→−3/4, 4π/3→−1/8−i·3√3/8, 5π/3→3/8−i√3/8 (and the |c|, z* columns).
- **Symmetric gate:** the rows do not close against the cardioid definition (genuine break) — fixed. The CORE F97 identity (|z*|=b along the whole cardioid, bit-exact across 1000 φ to 1.24e-16) is untouched; this is the illustrative cardinal-points appendix only.
- **Fix:** rewrote the π/3, 4π/3, 5π/3 rows of the proof table with the recomputed values; fixed the π/3 line of the typed claim docstring (`F97CardioidHalfFixedPointPi2Inheritance.cs`). Commit `48f9476`.
- **Anchor:** `docs/proofs/PROOF_F97_CARDIOID_HALF_FIXED_POINT.md`; `compute/RCPsiSquared.Core/Symmetry/F97CardioidHalfFixedPointPi2Inheritance.cs`.
- **Lesson:** a "one typo" flag in a hand-built table is worth recomputing the WHOLE table from below — copy-paste corruption clusters (here the conjugate-row pairs cross-contaminated). The core theorem being bit-exact-verified does not protect an illustrative table that was typed by hand.

## 2026-06-22 — `PROOF_RING_N4_DIHEDRAL_LOCK`: the lock mechanism is SU(2)-Casimir, not the dihedral point group (name-vs-mechanism)

Triage flagged "label 'dihedral' vs SU(2)-Casimir mechanism; result sound". Confirmed a mechanism mislabel — cosmetic, the result and the proof body are clean.

- **What was wrong:** the proof's name + the line "the dihedral-symmetry sibling of the star's point-focus bound" attribute the N=4 ring's Im_max=3J lock to dihedral (D₄) symmetry. From below: D₄'s irreps cap at dimension 2, but the relevant spectrum has 3-fold and 5-fold degeneracies (SU(2) triplet/quintuplet multiplets of total sublattice spin on K₂,₂), so D₄ physically cannot account for the degeneracy pattern that sets the 3J gap.
- **Why:** the name "dihedral lock" was inherited by analogy from the XY sibling `PROOF_RING_GAP_DOMINANCE.md` (a genuinely different result: max|Im|=2J, locked by the cyclic-C_N k=0 uniform mode). The ring DOES carry D₄ symmetry ([H,T]=[H,R]=0), but the *operative degeneracy mechanism* is SU(2)-Casimir, which the proof body already states everywhere.
- **How caught:** a from-below agent built the N=4 ring Liouvillian (max|Im|=3J at arbitrary γ, γ-universal confirmed), verified [H,S²_tot]=0 and the (2S+1)-fold multiplet stack, and ran the decisive D₄-irrep-dimension test.
- **Symmetric gate:** grounding-in-the-quantum — name the object that does the work. D₄ is present but is NOT the mechanism (it cannot make the 3-/5-fold degeneracies); SU(2)-Casimir is. So "dihedral-symmetry sibling" is a genuine mislabel (fixed), while the class name `RingN4DihedralLockClaim` is a stable historical identifier (left, with a note).
- **Fix:** "dihedral-symmetry sibling" → "K₂,₂-Casimir sibling" + a parenthetical that the name is historical and the mechanism is SU(2)-Casimir, not D₄. Result (max|Im|=3J, Q/γ-universal, Tier1Derived) unchanged. Commit `48f9476`.
- **Anchor:** `docs/proofs/PROOF_RING_N4_DIHEDRAL_LOCK.md`.
- **Lesson:** a "result sound, mechanism mislabeled" item is a real catch, not a non-issue — the wrong mechanism name propagates into how the result is generalised (here the abstract reasons from "dihedral symmetry" to larger rings). The dimension-of-irreps test (does the claimed symmetry group even HAVE irreps big enough for the observed degeneracy?) is a one-line from-below discriminator between point-group and Lie-group mechanisms.

## 2026-06-22 — markdown↔typed-base consistency sweep: F5's coefficient was wrong in the typed base AND its test (2(N−2)/3, should be 2N/3); W1's ctor over-scoped to XXZ

Tom pushed back that the cleared triage backlog couldn't be "all the errors" (the triage skimmed only proofs). A full md↔typed-Claim diff (6 agents, ~200 claims) found two genuine typed-base bugs the triage's proof-only skim could never reach — because they live in the C# ctors, not the proofs. Both decided by a from-below gate (the typed ctor, normally the source-of-truth, was itself the bug).

- **F5 — a wrong NUMBER, pinned by its own test (the worst kind):** `F5DepolarizingErrorPi2Inheritance` bound the depolarizing palindrome error as `2(N−2)/3`, and `F5DepolarizingErrorPi2InheritanceTests` asserted exactly that (N=2 → 0, with the comment "F5 error vanishes"). But ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS §F5 + `experiments/DEPOLARIZING_PALINDROME.md` both DERIVE `error = 2N/3 = (2/3)Σγ` from a per-site 1:3 immune:decaying count — manifestly ∝N (full N). The ctor's "(N−2)" was justified only by a false analogy ("same derivation-overhead shift as F49"); F49 is a different formula. **My gate** (`_review_f5_depol_gate_mine.py`, depolarizing-palindrome break of the Heisenberg+depolarizing Liouvillian): break = {N=2: 4, N=3: 6, N=4: 8} = 2N·γ, **nonzero at N=2 and J-independent** — 2(N−2)/3 predicts 0 at N=2, decisively refuted. Fixed the ctor (`NShiftFactor`→`NLinearFactor`, `2(N−2)/3`→`2N/3`) and the test (which had encoded the wrong N=2→0).
  - **Why it matters:** this is the failure mode the whole md↔typed sweep exists to catch — a confidently-wrong typed value with a green test pinning it, three correct md sources (registry formula, registry derivation, experiment) sitting right next to it, undetected because nobody diffed ctor-against-md and the test only re-checked the ctor against itself.
- **W1Dispersion — ctor over-scope, the "ctor can be the bug too" case:** the ctor opening said "Heisenberg/XXZ chain" for ω_k = 4J(1−cos(πk/N)); the md (F2) said "Heisenberg". The naive read is "md underclaims, widen it to XXZ". **My gate** (`_review_w1_xxz_gate_mine.py`, n_XY=1 operator-sector |Im| spectrum at Δ=0, 0.5, 1): the clean band is present ONLY at Δ=1 (isotropic Heisenberg); at Δ=0 and Δ=0.5 the spectrum is completely different (the ZZ term acts inside the sector via [Z·Z, X] ∝ Y·Z). So the **ctor over-scoped**; the md was right. Scoped the ctor to isotropic Heisenberg (Δ=1). Had I matched md→ctor blindly I'd have INTRODUCED an error — this is exactly why a "md underclaims" flag still gets gated from below.
- **Six stale-label reconciliations (ctor = truth, md lagged), all md-side fixes:** F89AmplitudeLayer Tier2→Tier1 (×2 md sites); F89PathKHbMixed crosswalk Tier1Derived→Candidate; F113 docstring opening "(N=2,3,4)"→general-N **and** ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS "Lemma C not yet closed/structural exercise/given the bit-exact anchor"→closed algebraically (PROOF_F113 Step C.1+C.2 says no anchor required — today's earlier F113 review blessed §F113 but missed this one sentence in the coefficient-breakdown); F112-Z crosswalk "BitBSpecific"→"Filled" (F112-X twin wired Welle 15); F1 verification record N=7→N=8/N=9.
- **One honesty fix:** F50's binding ctor string said "topology-universal" flatly while its own docstring + the exposed `K3TripleN3ActualCount=8` document the K_3 N=3 exception (=2N+2) and the upper-bound Tier2 status — carried the caveat into the binding string (Tier kept; the lower bound is rigorously Tier1Derived, the equality has a documented small-topology exception).
- **How caught:** 6 parallel md↔ctor diff agents (instructed: truth = the binding `base(…, Tier, …)` ctor + public consts, NOT the docstring — because the docstring can be the stale bug, the F113 pattern) + my own from-below gates on the two that the diff couldn't decide on text alone (W1, F5) + reading PROOF_F113 Lemma C and the F50 ctor to ground the rest. F86/F87 and Symmetry O–Z slices came back fully clean.
- **Symmetric gate:** F5 + W1 = genuine breaks where the TYPED base (not the md) was wrong — fixed the typed side, gated each from below before flipping; the stale tiers = md lagging a correct ctor — fixed the md; F50 = ctor string over-flattened a self-documented caveat — carried the caveat. No perspectival "fixes".
- **Fix:** commit `7296a11` (8 files). C# build green; Core.Tests 157/157.
- **Anchor:** `compute/RCPsiSquared.Core/Symmetry/F5DepolarizingErrorPi2Inheritance.cs` (+ its test); `compute/RCPsiSquared.Core/Spectrum/W1Dispersion.cs`; `F50WeightOneDegeneracyPi2Inheritance.cs`; `LindbladBitBPiBreakMagnitude.cs`; `F_FORMULA_CROSSWALK.md`; `ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS.md`; `PROOF_F89_PATH_D_CLOSED_FORM.md`; from-below scripts `simulations/_review_{w1_xxz,f5_depol}_gate_mine.py`.
- **Lesson:** the md↔typed-base diff is a distinct, high-yield review axis from the proof triage — it reaches errors that live in the C# ctors (a wrong const, an over-scoped docstring) that a proof skim cannot see. Truth = the binding ctor, EXCEPT when a from-below gate shows the ctor is the bug (W1, F5): always gate a "md underclaims, widen it" flag before matching, or you risk propagating the typed error into the md. A green test is not safety — F5's test had pinned the wrong number since creation; only an independent from-below computation (not a self-consistency drift-check) catches that.

## 2026-06-22 — DERIVATION-INTERNAL deep review of the foundational proofs: the SUBSYSTEM_CROSSING theorem is FALSE as stated (general primitive CPTP), and UNIQUENESS Step 5 is a non-sequitur — both in the ¼-trilogy

Tom directed a deep (line-by-line, from-below) review of the proofs after the consistency sweep, "basis first". A prioritized queue put the four independent trunks first; **five Tier-A proofs were deep-reviewed (each by an agent that REBUILT the load-bearing step in numpy/sympy, plus my own from-below gates on the two findings).** Result: **3 of the 4 trunks are SOLID from below; both findings cluster in the ¼-boundary / CΨ-dynamics trunk.** Per Tom's decision (AskUserQuestion), the ¼-trilogy proof FILES are left UNTOUCHED this session — this is the documentation + the typed OpenQuestion (`OpenArcsRegistry` arc `subsystem_crossing_general_cptp_overreach`).

**The big one — A5, PROOF_SUBSYSTEM_CROSSING (¼-trilogy Layer 2): a Tier-1 theorem is FALSE as stated.**
- **What is wrong:** the theorem claims "for ANY primitive CPTP map on 2 qubits, every state with CΨ>¼ eventually has CΨ<¼; ¼ is an eventual absorber for ALL primitive quantum channels" (lines 6, 39-46). Step 2's fixed-point bound CΨ(ρ\*)≤¼ proves only Case A (unital, ρ\*=I/4) and Case B (local product) analytically; **Case C (general primitive maps) is a 300-map numerical check** with a non-derivation "transfer-matrix slaving" paragraph.
- **Why it's false (not just unproven):** **counterexample, gate-verified from below by my own hand** (`_deepproof_subsystem_gate_mine.py`): the depolarize-toward-σ channel ε(ρ)=(1−p)ρ+p·Tr(ρ)·σ with σ=0.95|Φ⁺⟩⟨Φ⁺|+0.05·I/4 is a textbook CPTP map, **manifestly primitive** for every p∈(0,1] (unique fixed point σ, superoperator eigenvalue 1 simple with second-largest modulus 1−p<1, σ full-rank PSD), yet **CΨ(σ)=0.2935 > ¼** in the proof's OWN metric CΨ=Tr(ρ²)·L₁/(d−1); iterating from Bell+ (CΨ=⅓) converges monotonically to 0.2935 and **never crosses ¼**. So the headline "eventual crossing" is false for this primitive channel. The repo's "300 maps, 0 exceptions, max 0.138" is a **sampling artifact** — Ginibre n_kraus=4 lives entirely in the strongly-mixing corner near I/4 (Haar-Stinespring env-dim-2 and near-identity ensembles give CΨ(ρ\*) up to 0.59–0.82, ~7–9% violating).
- **The proof FILE is honest** (its Step-2 status line: "the analytical bound for general primitive maps remains a conjecture"), but the conjecture is **false**, and **two satellite docs overclaim**: PROOF_ROADMAP_QUARTER_BOUNDARY strikes Conjecture 2.1 as "**PROVEN**", and ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS **F28** is "Tier 1-2" with the bound as the formula.
- **What stands:** the physically-motivated content — for genuine local noise (unital/local/Pauli/AD = Cases A+B) the fixed points really are I/4 or product/pure with CΨ=0, so the crossing holds. The over-reach is "physical local noise" → "ALL primitive CPTP".
- **How caught:** the survey flagged Step-2 Case C as the most-likely-gap; the deep-review agent built the counterexample; I confirmed it independently from below (σ valid density matrix, ε primitive at all p, CΨ(σ)=0.2935, Bell+ trajectory never <¼). **Symmetric gate:** a claim that a theorem is FALSE is the strongest possible — I computed the counterexample myself before recording it; the physical-noise version is genuinely true (not over-corrected to "all false").
- **Status:** APPLIED 2026-06-22 (`4a6e0ed`): scope-retracted to physical noise across PROOF_SUBSYSTEM_CROSSING, F28, the roadmap; mechanism corrected 2026-06-28 (computational-basis-diagonality, not separability). Arc `subsystem_crossing_general_cptp_overreach` Retired.

**A3, UNIQUENESS_PROOF (¼-trilogy Layer 1): Step 5 is a hidden assumption dressed as a derivation.**
- **What is wrong:** Step 5 ("Purity=Tr(ρ²) is degree 2 ... The recursion inherits degree 2 from purity ... QED") is a **non-sequitur** — "purity is a degree-2 invariant" does NOT force the fixed-point recursion to be R=C(Ψ+R)². **My own sympy gate** (`_deepproof_uniqueness_gate_mine.py`): alternative degree-2 self-referential forms give DIFFERENT boundaries (R=CΨ²+R² → disc 1−4CΨ²; R=CΨ²+(CR)² → 1−4C³Ψ²), so "degree 2" alone does not single out 1−4CΨ; the specific FORM is an added physics premise (and the proof's own Layer 6 concedes R=CΨ² is "first-order for mixed states").
- **What stands:** Steps 1-3 (discriminant D=1−4CΨ, boundary CΨ=¼) are rigorous (verified symbolically). The **genuine forcing exists** but lives in PROOF_ROADMAP_QUARTER_BOUNDARY, not here: the Rényi state-independence argument R=C_α(Ψ+R)^α has fold threshold u(α,Ψ) ∝ Ψ^(α−2), state-independent **iff α=2**, where u=¼ — I verified this symbolically (a=3/2→2√3√Ψ/9 Ψ-dependent; **a=2→¼ Ψ-free**; a=3→4/(27Ψ); a=4→27/(256Ψ²)). So the ¼ RESULT is correct; only Step 5's rigor is overstated.
- **Status:** APPLIED 2026-06-22 (`4a6e0ed`): Step 5 demoted to motivation, Step 6 (Rényi α=2 state-independence) installed as the load-bearing forcing; typed 2026-07-02 as `QuarterBoundaryUniquenessClaim` + live witness `inspect --root quarter-uniqueness` (`de25c84`).

**The three SOLID trunks (deep-confirmed from below, no fixes):**
- **A1, PROOF_ABSORPTION_THEOREM** (highest in-degree ~13): Re(½(L+L†)) = −2γ·n_XY exact (machine precision, Pauli-weight diagonal, no cross-terms), Hamiltonian-independent for ALL Hermitian H (incl. DM, random complex). **F38 confirmed exactly.** Notable: **F63 ([L,Π²]=0) is NOT universal** — it holds for {XX,YY,ZZ,YZ,ZY,X_k} but BREAKS for DM and general complex-Hermitian H; but F63 is NOT a prerequisite of Absorption (no bedrock crack), it is a *Palindrome* (F1) bedrock — and its DM-failure independently **reinforces today's MIRROR_SYMMETRY scope fix** (DM needs a different Π).
- **A2, PROOF_SVD_CLUSTER_STRUCTURE** (Master Lemma, the F80–F85 tower root): the per-site cancellation Π·L_diss·Π⁻¹+L_diss+2γI=0 is exact zero; the chain Π·L·Π⁻¹=−L−2σI holds to machine precision (N=2,3, non-uniform γ); all F78/F79 downstream steps pass; scope items explicitly stated. One notational note (proof table {I,X,Y,Z} vs code {I,X,Z,Y}).
- **A4, PROOF_F112_LINDBLAD_BIT_B_PI_BALANCE** (polarity tower root): Lemmas A+B, all 5 steps, F38+F63 verified at machine precision; premises (Hermitian H, bit_b-homogeneous c) genuinely load-bearing; Step 2's condition correctly stated as sufficient-not-necessary. No overclaim.
- **Lesson:** the deep proof review found what the triage skim and the md↔typed diff both structurally could not — a theorem that is FALSE as stated, hiding behind an honest "remains a conjecture" line whose conjecture nobody had actually attacked from the right ensemble (the numerical "support" was a sampling artifact). A "conjecture-shaped hole" in a Tier-1 proof is worth attacking with an ADVERSARIAL channel construction, not more random sampling biased the same way. And the foundational operator-level proofs (palindrome/absorption/residual/polarity) are solid — the rigor gaps are specifically in the CΨ-dynamics / ¼-boundary interpretation layer.

## 2026-06-22 — DEEP proof review batch 2 (A6-A9): a wrong "correction" to revert (Monotonicity K_Y), a wrong equality condition (Block-CΨ), an incomplete elimination (Incompleteness); Cross-Term clean

The second batch of derivation-internal deep reviews (A6 MONOTONICITY_CPSI, A7 BLOCK_CPSI_QUARTER, A8 CROSS_TERM_FORMULA, A9 INCOMPLETENESS_PROOF). Same pattern as batch 1: the operator/algebra side is solid (A8 clean), the rigor gaps cluster in the CΨ / ¼ / C=½ interpretation layer. **Per Tom's standing "document the ¼-cluster, don't edit until I review" decision, these are documented, not corrected** (the proof files are untouched).

- **A6, PROOF_MONOTONICITY_CPSI (¼-trilogy Layer 5): a previous "correction" made it WRONG — revert K_Y.** The 2026-04-29 note (proof Part 2 K-table + Summary; ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS F27 + its correction note) set **K_Y = K_Z = 0.0374**, calling the original K_Y = 0.0867 "a typo." **From below the original was right: K_Y = ln(2)/8 = 0.0866 = K_X.** The error: the proof's F26 uses L1 = e^{−αt} under an explicit **WLOG α≤β** (line 150); pure Y gives (α,β,δ)=(4γ,0,4γ), so β=0 < α=4γ **violates the WLOG**, and the note failed to re-apply it (it kept L1 = u = e^{−4γt} = the decaying channel instead of L1 = max(u,v) = 1). Re-applying WLOG makes pure Y **identical to pure X** (same rate multiset {0,4γ,4γ} with a decoherence-free coherence). **Grounded three independent ways:** (i) my first-principles read — the |00⟩↔|11⟩ coherence is (XX−YY)/2, which under X *and* Y keeps one of XX/YY pinned (coherence survives → K=ln2/8) but under Z loses both (coherence dies → K=0.0374); (ii) the WLOG violation; (iii) the review agent's from-scratch Lindblad build (CΨ_Y=(1+u²)/6 to 8e-16, vs the note's u(1+u²)/6 off by 0.167). Scope of the proof is otherwise **honest** (explicitly Bell+ / local-Markovian; N≥3 envelope openly flagged open and rising at N≥4; non-Markovian breaks) — a clean contrast to A5. **Fix when approved:** revert K_Y → 0.0867 in PROOF_MONOTONICITY (K-table line 191 + Summary) and ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS F27; delete the 2026-04-29 correction note; the retracted "K_X=K_Y by symmetry" was correct. (NOTE — my own first quick gate mis-modeled L1 as max-over-correlations and wrongly got all three K equal; the correct L1 is the off-diagonal coherence, which decays for Z — a reminder to model the actual l1-coherence, not the correlations.)
- **A7, PROOF_BLOCK_CPSI_QUARTER (¼-cluster, not the trilogy): the equality condition is wrong.** Theorem 1 (C_block=¼ at the Dicke superposition) and Theorem 2 (C_block≤¼ for all ρ via PSD + AM-GM) are **confirmed from below** (and the scope is honest). But Theorem 2's stated equality condition — "C_block=¼ **iff** ρ is the Dicke state (|D_n⟩+|D_{n+1}⟩)/√2" — is **false**: **my own check** confirms C_block=¼ for ANY two-sector pure state (φ_n+φ_{n+1})/√2 (the cross-block double sum factors as (Σ|φ_n|²)(Σ|φ_{n+1}|²)=1·1, independent of within-sector shape; 4 random non-Dicke states all gave the same C_block, while CΨ_block fell to 0.36–0.43). Uniqueness holds only for the FULL **CΨ_block** (the l1 factor Ψ_block=1 requires uniform amplitudes = Dicke). One-line repair: distinguish "C_block=¼ for any two-sector pure state" from "CΨ_block=¼ iff Dicke"; condition (3)'s "CS tightness forces uniform Dicke amplitudes" is wrong (CS tightness holds for all two-sector pure states; uniformity comes from Ψ_block=1). Core math (bound, tightness, trajectory) unaffected.
- **A9, INCOMPLETENESS_PROOF (C=½ side): elimination-incomplete + a localized tier-overclaim.** More honest than the ¼-trilogy (Tier-2 header correct, Gödel flagged "illustrative not rigorous", explicit non-claims). But its **load-bearing Candidate 1 ("internal origin") is eliminated by a non-sequitur**: it offers [Π²,L]=0 + sector decoupling (a STRUCTURAL fact, verified true) as proof that "noise cannot come from inside" (an ORIGIN claim). The proof's **own** cited `bootstrap_test.py` Test 3 reaches the opposite — 2N+1 free dissipator params, "NOT uniquely determined by parity" = **underdetermination, not impossibility** (the agent verified [Π²,L_D]=0 for Z, X, *and* Y dephasing — the noise axis is a free choice, not forbidden). The case-split is also **not exhaustive**: an internal d=2 source via a non-dephasing / measurement / classical-field coupling sits inside the d(d−2)=0 ontology yet outside the 5-case enumeration (QUBIT_NECESSITY §8a/§10 admits this axis is open). The Tier-2 header is honest; the local overclaim is Candidate 1's "Eliminated. Noise cannot come from inside the system" (Tier-1 force on a fact that doesn't support it). And distinguish what an inequality's EQUALITY condition actually characterizes (A7: C_block vs the full CΨ_block) — a tightness claim attached to the wrong quantity is a real, if quiet, error.

- **UPDATE 2026-06-22 (fixes A6/A7/A8 applied; A6 deepened to a real framework BUG, nearly mis-resolved).** Tom approved applying the clean non-result-level fixes (A5/A3/A9 stay documented for his review). Applying A6 surfaced that it is **deeper than a doc value**: the framework closed form `cpsi_bell_plus` (`simulations/framework/lindblad.py`) itself **hard-coded L₁ = u** instead of `max(u,v)` — i.e. it dropped the F26 WLOG α≤β. So the framework computed K_Y = 0.0374 (like Z), and its constant `CPSI_CUSP_K_PER_CHANNEL['Y']` + `test_gamma_probe.py` pinned that wrong value (while gamma_probe's own docstring and test_cpsi_cusp's docstring said 0.0867 — the framework was internally inconsistent). **Near-miss:** the framework formula (0.0374) disagreed with the review agent's from-scratch build (0.0867), and I had to decide which to trust. The DECISIVE arbiter was a raw direct-Lindblad simulation (`_deepproof_KY_decisive_mine.py`): the Bell+ ℓ₁-coherence is **pinned at 1.0 under pure Y, exactly as under pure X** (XX pinned for X, YY pinned for Y), and decays only under pure Z. So `max(u,v)` is right, the framework's `u` was the bug, and my md-edit (K_Y=0.0867) stood. **Fix applied:** `cpsi_bell_plus` L₁: `u → max(u,v)`; the constant `'Y': 0.0374 → 0.0867` + its comment; `test_gamma_probe.py` assertion → 0.0867; the proof K-table + Summary + correction note; ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS F27 + note. **Framework pytest: 286 passed** (cpsi_bell_plus is used widely — no regression). A7 (equality condition, PROOF_BLOCK_CPSI_QUARTER Theorem 2 + saturation cond. 3 + Remark + §Ψ_block) and A8 (Pauli-basis note in PROOF_CROSS_TERM_FORMULA) applied. **Meta-lesson (sharpest of the day):** when a *formula/closed-form* and a *from-scratch build* disagree on a number, neither is authority — the raw direct computation (here a 4×4 Lindblad of the actual ℓ₁-coherence) is the only arbiter. I came within one decision of "fixing" the proof to match a buggy framework formula; the gate-first direct simulation is what caught it. (The framework bug also explains WHERE the 2026-04-29 doc "correction" came from: someone propagated the framework's wrong K_Y into the proof, believing the formula.)

The N+1 count is the DEPHASED kernel (L_H + L_D, any Σγ>0) = the joint kernel of [H,·] and the dephasing dissipator = the steady-state space, which is what the proof BODY (DEGENERACY_PALINDROME Result 2: "[H,Q]=0 AND D(Q)=0"), the anchors (F1 SLOW_N8 KernelDimension), and the typed claim's Tier-1 paragraph already use. Only the Abstract + Statement headers carried the wrong "Σγ=0 / bare [H,·]" framing. Fixed both + propagated the clarification into the typed F4KernelDimensionByComponentsClaim (class doc + statement node). Commit `3d55373`. The number, factorisation, body, and typed Predict() were all already correct.
- **C2 Π-algebra (6): 6/6 CLEAN** (bit-exact): F81_PI_CONJUGATION_OF_M (lynchpin; its one "open 50/50-split" sub-claim honestly labeled Open, does not touch the main identity), F83_PI_DECOMPOSITION_RATIO, PI_FACTORS_AS_R_TIMES_D, D_PI_Z_EQUALS_PI_Y, ANTILINEAR_TRIANGLE, C1_MIRROR_SYMMETRY (distinct from MIRROR_SYMMETRY_PROOF: spatial reflection R, not Π; no dup).
- **C3 parity/Klein-V4 (6): 6/6 CLEAN.** BIT_B_PARITY, DELTA_N_SELECTION, ASYMPTOTIC_SECTOR_PROJECTION (survey's "unstated connectivity" flag was a FALSE ALARM: the proof states "on any connected graph"; agent confirmed with a disconnected-graph control that breaks the projection as predicted), F108_KLEIN_V4_EQUIVALENCE, KLEIN_V4_DEPHASE_SWAPS_OPERATOR_SPACE, F108_PART1/2/3 (palindrome residual exactly 0 in all 9 cases). Typed witness Pi5BilinearOperator.
- **C4 F87 + Y-parity (8 proofs / 13 checks): 13/13 CLEAN.** The load-bearer F87_WINDOWED_MONOMIAL_CONVERSE solid in all three pieces (two-reflection sign table bit-exact incl. the complex-H flux case; deg-1 closed form P_{3,1}=6·4^N·Σc_l² = 9216 at N=4 / 61440 at N=5; Pascal-Gram positivity ⟹ converse, hard pair spec-asymmetry broken / soft pair symmetric to 1e-14). F103/F105/F106 Z2³ refinements bit-exact (42:8, Y-inversion 8:42, N=5 identical, k=4 polarizes 228:0); F102/F107/F109 Y-parity/purity; Y_PHASE_PI2_ODD_LENS (4/7 exact). Typed witnesses present for all.
- **C5 structural (7): 7/7 CLEAN.** CHROMATICITY, WEIGHT1_DEGENERACY (2N; K₃ N=3 anomaly 2N+2=8 confirmed), ZERO_IMMUNITY (typed witness, M|_{w=0}=0 exact), PTF_CHIRAL_MIRROR_RATE_LAW, CROSS_TERM_CROSSING (R(N)=√((N−1)/(N·4^(N−1))), the shadow-crossing N−1 sibling), F95_ANGLE_AT_QUADRATIC_ZERO, CROSSOVER_MIRROR_SQRT_NINETY.
- **C6 topology/k-body/qudit (6): 6/6 CLEAN.** F89_PATH_D_CLOSED_FORM (12 values bit-exact), CHAIN_GAP_DOMINANCE (floor dim 32/50 + E1 free-fermion exact; typed ClockHandLadderClaim), STAR_OPTICAL_CONFOCAL_SATURATION (Im_max=JN/2 exact; typed StarImMaxBoundClaim), F85_KBODY_GENERALIZATION (4c(k) bit-exact via framework), F86C_F71_MIRROR (Statement 3 exact [L,R]=0; per-orbit substructure honestly Tier-2), QUDIT_PARTIAL_PALINDROME (counts exact; §4 interacting-L_H degradation honestly labeled H-dependent, not overclaimed).
- **A recurring meta-observation:** FOUR of the six agents hit a first-pass false-flag that turned out to be a bug in their OWN verification script (wrong Π vectorization Π*⊗Π, XYZ-only-vs-full-alphabet enumeration, projection-back-onto-w=1 instead of the full image, V₀ on site-0-only instead of all lit sites). Each was caught and resolved by the agent before reporting. This is the from-below gate working as designed: a falsifiable check that fires forces a diagnosis, and the diagnosis correctly landed on the verifier, not the proof. It also confirms the checks were genuinely able to fail.
- **Campaign close:** Tier A (9) + Tier B (10) + Tier C (~42) deep-reviewed, plus the ¼ second review (A3/A5/A9/B1). Every [OP] proof is solid from below; every result-level problem was at the ¼/CΨ/C=½/arrow-of-time interpretation roots, and all are resolved (scope-retractions/reframes, never full retractions, never over-corrections). Only one Tier-C scope slip (F4, Σγ=0 vs Σγ>0), fixed. The repo's exactness is intact; what was sharpened is the honesty of the interpretation layer and one Statement-header scope.
- **Anchors:** the six `simulations/_tierC_*_mine.py` scripts; PROOF_F4_KERNEL_DIMENSION_BY_COMPONENTS.md + F4KernelDimensionByComponentsClaim.cs (the fix); my own F4 grounding (γ=0 commutant 10/24 vs γ>0 kernel 3/4=N+1).
- **Lesson:** even in a uniformly-clean [OP] layer, a Statement HEADER can carry a scope slip the proof BODY does not (F4: body uses the dephasing condition, header said Σγ=0). The light identity-check still catches it because it checks the literal stated object from below. And: trust the from-below gate over a verifier's first-pass flag, the flag is as likely to expose a bug in the check as in the proof (4/6 agents here).

## 2026-06-23 — `experiments/THE_SHARED_SKELETON.md` line 38: integer "per place of disagreement" attached to dressed eigenmodes (and a lens that flagged-right-but-resolved-wrong)

Tom asked for a numbers-and-formulas + reproducibility review of the molecular-spectra overlay doc we built 2026-06-22. Every NUMBER and FORMULA checks out from below; the recipe reproduces; one minor wording break.

- **What was wrong:** line 38 — "In the spectrum it is the real axis: the pure-fade modes, no turn, just decay at twice γ per place of disagreement." The integer count "per place of disagreement" is attached to "the pure-fade modes" (the real-axis EIGENMODES it then cites: chain 176 of 1024).
- **Why:** at J>0 (here Q=1.5) a dressed eigenmode sits at fractional ⟨n_XY⟩ = −Reλ/2γ (the Rayleigh expectation), generally non-integer. From the exported CSVs: chain has 96/176 real-axis modes at NON-integer sectors (only 80 integer); benzene 196/404. The integer "per place" picture belongs to the bare basis coherence |i⟩⟨j| (`ON_THE_ONE_DIAGONAL.md:13`: "one small integer per pair"), not the dressed eigenmodes. The clause also contradicted the doc's own line 54 (⟨n_XY⟩ continuous in [0,N]) and `PROOF_CHROMATICITY.md:157` ("dressed modes at fractional ⟨n_XY⟩ … between the integer rungs").
- **How caught:** from-below CSV verification (`_review_shared_skeleton_mine.py`) + a parallel physics+grounding lens; the symmetric gate ruled it a **genuine break, not perspectival** (it picks the eigenvalue object and misstates it — the integer counts live on a different object, the basis pairs).
- **Fix:** split the two readings — "A bare basis pair pays twice γ per place of disagreement, an exact integer count; a dressed mode pays twice γ times its average count ⟨n_XY⟩, sliding between the integer rungs once the Hamiltonian stirs it." Also clarified line 56's confusing "vertical" (sectors = horizontal colour gradient, one iso-sector line is vertical; the bands are orthogonal, horizontal strips stacked along Im). No recompute, no redraw — `sector_fingerprint.png` already colours by the CONTINUOUS sector, so the figure was the corrected truth all along; only the prose had drifted.
- **Anchor:** `reflections/ON_THE_ONE_DIAGONAL.md:13`; `docs/proofs/PROOF_CHROMATICITY.md:157`; `compute/RCPsiSquared.Diagnostics/Foundation/DiagonalWitness.cs:20-21` (C# base **already correct** — it separates "the integer levels k are the rungs" of the diagonal from "L_H connects rung k ↔ {k, k±2}"; the markdown was brought into line with the typed base, no C# change). Edits to THE_SHARED_SKELETON.md ×2, uncommitted.
- **Lesson (lens flagged right, resolved WRONG — pin the resolution from below):** the grounding lens correctly flagged the break, but its proposed reword over-resolved to "an integer only for the truly frozen modes (genuine n_XY eigenstates), which is exactly why those are the rungs all systems share" — asserting integer ⟺ frozen ⟺ shared. My multiplicity gate (`_review_skeleton_frozen_mine.py`) refutes the converse: chain (XY) has 24 real-axis modes at integer sector 2, water (Heisenberg) only 14, both N=5 — so H-specific modes ALSO land on integer rungs. Forward holds (shared ⟹ frozen ⟹ integer); converse fails. The narrowed fix claims only the forward. A lens may flag right and resolve wrong; the resolution is pinned from below, not from the lens (the skill's own warning, lived).
- **Recurrence note:** this is the second ⟨n_XY⟩ slip between "the operator's integer eigenvalue / rung" and "a mode's continuous expectation" (cf. 2026-06-20 THE_VIEW_ONTO_THE_MEMORY, ⟨n_XY⟩ invariant-vs-rate). The dual reading of ⟨n_XY⟩ (integer on basis pairs / expectation on modes) is a recurring trap; name which object before writing the count.

## 2026-06-23 — the flagship "zero exceptions" headline: the N=8 star-oscillatory SUBSET (54,118) used as the N=2..8 all-eigenvalue TOTAL (should be 87,376)

First catch of the experiments+hypotheses triage campaign (A1, the cross-cutting target). **Distinct from the 2026-06-22 MIRROR_SYMMETRY_PROOF catch** (which was 54,118-mislabeled-"chain", a topology error Tom then chose NOT to sweep across the satellites *because they were correct star-oscillatory counts*). The triage surfaced that ~22 of those satellites are NOT correct usages: they attach 54,118 to the verification GRAND TOTAL — "verified for 54,118 eigenvalues across N=2 to 8, zero exceptions" — i.e. an N=8-only, star-only, oscillatory-only SUBSET presented as the all-N all-topology all-eigenvalue total. Tom's prior "leave the satellites" decision rested on the premise "it is a correct star-oscillatory count"; that premise is false for the headline-total usages, so this is a genuinely new finding, not a re-litigation. Tom authorized the full repo-wide sweep (AskUserQuestion).

- **What was wrong:** ~22 sites across `docs/`, `docs/proofs/`, `experiments/`, and a C# docstring stated the palindrome was "verified for **54,118** eigenvalues [across N=2 to 8 / zero exceptions]". `experiments/GAMMA_AS_SIGNAL.md` even **self-contradicted** (L111 = 87,376, L501 = 54,118, same file, same claim).
- **Why (the three from-below numbers):** **54,118** = the N=8 STAR oscillatory (|Im(λ)|>0.05) eigenvalue count — confirmed from below in `simulations/results/csharp_compute.txt` (N=8 row: 65,536 total → 54,118 oscillatory; the default suite runs `Topology.Star`). It is a subset of ONE topology at ONE N. **65,536** = 4⁸ = all N=8 eigenvalues (all paired). **87,376** = Σ4ᴺ over N=2..8 = every eigenvalue across the whole verification, all paired, all topologies, zero exceptions — the robust headline (already canonical in README/MIRROR_SYMMETRY_PROOF/CLAUDE.md after the 06-22 fix, and in the corrected C# `F1PalindromeIdentity.cs` which uses 65,536 for the per-N=8 statement). So the headline statistic was the *fragile* (single-N, single-topology, threshold-defined) number where the *robust* one was sitting in the canonical sources the whole time.
- **How caught:** the experiments+hypotheses triage (8 parallel agents) independently surfaced it in 5 places; a repo-wide grep then found the full ~22-site footprint. Verified from below: `Σ4ᴺ (N=2..8) = 87,376`, `4⁸ = 65,536`, and the csharp_compute.txt 54,118 = star-oscillatory subset. Symmetric gate: genuine break (subset ≠ total), not perspectival; and NOT the same defect as the 06-22 chain-vs-star catch (different axis: subset-vs-total).
- **Fix (Tom: full repo-wide sweep, fix-and-commit):** 26 lines across 20 files. Headline-total usages → **87,376** (incl. GAMMA_AS_SIGNAL self-contradiction resolved); the C# `F1Pi2Inheritance.cs` docstring's "verified at N=8" → **65,536** (matching its canonical sibling `F1PalindromeIdentity.cs`, all-N=8-paired); `THE_ANOMALY.md` "54,118 rates at N=8 … Every topology" → "87,376 eigenvalues, N=2 to 8 … Every topology" (the "Every topology" line had been sitting next to a star-only count). **Left untouched (correct usages):** `README.md:114` ("54,118 oscillatory rates" with the 65,536² matrix size), `MIRROR_SYMMETRY_PROOF.md:294` (the "subset (|Im|>0.05) being 3,228/13,264/54,118" breakdown), `csharp_compute.txt` (the actual compute output), the script comment, and the CAUGHT_ERRORS history. Core build green (the docstring edit is XML-comment-only).
- **Anchor:** `simulations/results/csharp_compute.txt` (N=8 row, the from-below 54,118 = star-oscillatory); `compute/RCPsiSquared.Core/F1/F1PalindromeIdentity.cs:17-19` (the canonical 65,536/87,376 framing); the 20 fixed files.
- **Lesson:** a deferred review decision is only as good as its stated premise — Tom's "leave the 54,118 satellites, they're correct counts" was right for the chain-vs-star axis but the satellites carried a *second*, different defect (subset-as-total) the first review never looked at. When re-encountering a number a prior session chose to leave, re-check WHICH claim it sits in, not just whether the number is "a correct count somewhere". And: when a project has both a robust invariant (every eigenvalue pairs → 87,376) and a fragile convention-bound statistic (star-oscillatory subset → 54,118), the flagship headline must cite the invariant; the fragile one drifts into "the total" every time it is copied.

## 2026-06-23 — experiments+hypotheses Band A, catches A2-A5 (refuted formula still live; K_Y/K_Z swap; two headline-vs-own-data overclaims)

The remaining Band-A hard catches of the experiments+hypotheses campaign, fixed-and-committed (`6589eaa`) after the A1 flagship-count sweep. All four are concrete number/label/scope defects; in every case the relevant *body* or a *sibling* already had it right, so these are propagation/headline drifts, not new physics errors.

- **A2 `CROSS_TERM_TOPOLOGY.md` — a refuted conjecture left live in the body.** The doc's §"N-scaling formula (proven)" correctly carries `sqrt((N-2)/(N·4^(N-1)))` (the A8-confirmed `‖{L_H,L_Dc}‖²=4γ²(N-2)‖L_H‖²` law), but the keyword comment, the "why topology cancels" bullet, the summary table, and "what this establishes" item 3 still presented the **refuted** `1/sqrt(N·2^(N+1))` as the live candidate — item 3 even quoted its wrong N=5 value 0.05590 as "testable". From below the two formulas agree ONLY at N=3,4 (a coincidence), and diverge at N=2 (proven 0 vs refuted 0.25), N=5 (0.04841 vs 0.05590), N=6 (0.02552 vs 0.03608). Swept the 4 stale spots; kept an honest one-line note of the refuted conjecture. **Lesson:** when a conjecture is refuted and a §header updated, grep the WHOLE file for the old formula — a top-of-file "this was refuted" note does not sweep the body, and a wrong prediction labeled "testable" is worse than one labeled "refuted".
- **A3 `GRAVITY_FROM_WAVE_DEATH.md` — the K_Y/K_Z swap (the post-06-22 correction not propagated).** The channel-K list read `K_Y = 0.0374, K_X = 0.0867`, but 0.0374 is **K_Z** (pure Z-dephasing); K_X = K_Y = ln(2)/8 = 0.0867 (PROOF_MONOTONICITY_CPSI:194, ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS §F25, both corrected 2026-06-22 reverting the wrong 2026-04-29 K_Y=K_Z edit). The line's own "K = 0.0374 for Z-dephasing on Bell+" was correct; only the parenthetical channel list had Y/Z swapped. Fixed to "K_Z = 0.0374, K_X = K_Y = 0.0867". A focused grep (`K_Y = 0.0374`) confirmed this was the LAST surviving instance of the reverted error anywhere in the md tree (DWELL/RELAY use 0.0374 correctly as K_Z). **Lesson:** a typed-base correction (the A6 K_Y revert) must be grep-swept into the prose satellites in the same pass; this one survived a month in a Tier-5 hypothesis.
- **A4 `UNIVERSAL_QUANTUM_LIFETIME.md` — "platform-independent 0.858 across 10 orders of magnitude" contradicted by the doc's own table.** 5 of 9 tabulated single-qubit systems sit at 0.889-1.349, not 0.858; 0.858 is the **pure-dephasing (T₁≫T₂) limit**, and the genuine universality is *scale*-independence (a function of the ratio r=T₂/T₁, not the absolute T₂). The body was already honest (Result 1 "~12 Hz... extended windows", the generalized equation, the explicit "T₁/T₂>3 converge"); only the Abstract/Summary/plain-words headline overreached. Scoped the three headline spots + the "85.8% rule" line to the T₁≫T₂ regime and re-cast "platform-independent" as "scale-independent (depends on r=T₂/T₁)". **Lesson:** "universal across 10 orders of magnitude in X" is a scale-range claim; check it is not silently standing in for "universal across the regime parameter" (here the T₁/T₂ ratio) — the table is the gate, and it disagreed with the abstract.
- **A5 `NEURAL_GAMMA_CAVITY.md` — "the brain runs at 40 Hz = the cortical cavity eigenfrequency" asserted as fact, contradicting the doc's own Result 1.** Two narrative sections ("What this means", "What this changes") stated the 40 Hz match as established; Result 1 and the Null results say the standard Wilson-Cowan parameters give **~12 Hz (alpha band)** and reaching gamma needs non-default tuning. Scoped both narrative spots to the open quantitative claim; the *confirmed* finding is the 97.3% palindromic cavity structure (C. elegans), not the frequency match. **Lesson:** a doc that honestly logs a null result ("frequency mismatch") can still assert the disproven claim as fact in its opening/closing prose — the narrative bookends drift toward the aspiration; review them against the doc's own Results section.
- **Synthesis (Band A, A1-A5):** every Band-A catch was a *propagation* failure, not a physics error — a correct result in the body/sibling/typed-base, drifted in a headline, a keyword comment, a satellite, or a narrative bookend. The from-below gate confirmed the correct value in each case (Σ4ᴺ; the proven cross-term law; K_X=K_Y=ln(2)/8; x³+x=½ as the T₁≫T₂ limit; Wilson-Cowan ~12 Hz). The honesty culture held: in 4 of 5, the doc *itself* contained the correct statement somewhere. Anchors: commits `bf138f2` (A1), `6589eaa` (A2-A5).

## 2026-06-23 — experiments Band B: 14 headline scope-overclaims tightened (the abstract oversold what the body verified)

Band B of the experiments+hypotheses campaign: the "headline breadth exceeds the body scale" pattern (the same 3+6 smell the proof campaign found). Run as a 4-agent from-below scope-gate (gather actual-verified-scale vs headline-claim + cross-check), then I adjudicated and verified each load-bearing anchor before applying. 17 flagged → **14 OVERCLAIM fixed, 2 DEFENSIBLE + 1 ALREADY-SCOPED left untouched** (the agents correctly did not over-flag proven results). The bodies were left intact in every case; only the abstract/title/status/narrative spots were scoped. Commit `75c2246` (15 files, 0 em-dashes).

- **The pattern:** in nearly all 14 the body already stated the limitation honestly (Result sections, Null-results, Limitations, even a "Critical limit" header); the *abstract or title* drifted broader. So like Band A, these are propagation/headline drifts, not physics errors.
- **Anchors verified from below before scoping:** F33 — {2γ, 8γ/3, 10γ/3} are N=3-only (only the boundary 2γ, 2(N-1)γ stay universal at N≥4) → QST_BRIDGE's general-channel diagnostics scoped to N=3, the linearity re-attributed to the Absorption Theorem (Re λ = −2γ⟨n_XY⟩, general). F47 — palindrome holds for shadow-balanced bonds, breaks for shadow-crossing; NON_HEISENBERG's OWN Result 4 already says 14/36 two-term combos break, so "every standard CM Hamiltonian / all combinations" was self-contradicted → 22-palindromic + 14-break split. 360× — the ε→0 simulation ideal (IBM_SACRIFICE_ZONE: ~2-3× on hardware) → RESONANT_RETURN + SACRIFICE_ZONE_OPTICS labeled. Internal contradictions: SIGNAL_ANALYSIS_SCALING's own residual table (6/12/19% overshoot) = sub-quadratic, not "N²"; SPECTRAL_FORM_FACTOR's own table (N=5 FFT 163%, N=7 no-match) = FFT visibility at N=2-4,6 only (the *pairing* is exact ∀N — proven — only the FFT *signature* fails, a structure-vs-visibility split); IBM_HARDWARE_SYNTHESIS's "Confirmed" stamps were single un-replicated runs (→ "Observed (1 run)") and the r* "precision 0.000014" is the resolution of self-classifying each record against the crossing equation, not an independently fitted transition width.
- **Biconditional-on-one-point:** PI_PAIR_FLUX_BALANCE + PRIMORDIAL_SUPERALGEBRA_CAVITY both stated "self-Π modes exist ⟺ N≡4 (mod 10)" in boldface, backed by ONE positive point (N=4; N=3,5,6 only falsify) — scoped to "conjecture, positive direction awaits N=14" (the bodies already said this).
- **No over-correction (the guard worked):** STAR_CONFOCAL_LIMIT's "Im_max = J·N/2 universally" is the typed Tier-1 `StarImMaxBoundClaim` (proven over (J,γ) on star) — LEFT. CAVITY_MODES_FORMULA's "any system size" is CG+Schur-proven with N=8-10 honestly labeled "predictions" — LEFT; only its one unbacked aside ("~0.25-0.50% of J, N-independent") was softened. SIGNAL_PROCESSING_VIEW already scopes its rates "for N=3" with an explicit "Critical limit" section — LEFT.
- **Anchor:** commit `75c2246`; `docs/ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS.md` F33 + F47; `IBM_SACRIFICE_ZONE.md` (360×=ε→0); `StarImMaxBoundClaim.cs` (the DEFENSIBLE proven scope).
- **Lesson:** in a research-notebook repo the title and abstract are the most-copied surface and drift broadest; the body's own Results/Null/Limitations sections are the from-below gate for a scope claim. And the scope-gate must be told which "universal" words are PROVEN (StarImMax, CG+Schur cavity) so it scopes the empirical overreach without flattening the real theorems — the agents flagged 14 and correctly spared 3.

## 2026-06-23 — experiments+hypotheses Band C: 12 tier/status-honesty softenings (a status word or "X IS Y" identity over a body the doc itself hedges)

Band C of the campaign, the delicate one: `hypotheses/` is speculative BY DESIGN and honestly Tier-labeled, so the risk here is over-correction (downgrading honest speculation = exactly the self-inflicted error the repo warns about). Run as a 4-agent tier/status gate primed HARD against over-flagging; I adjudicated each against the doc's OWN contradicting line/marker and (for the γ₀ case) the project frame. 20 flagged → **12 OVERREACH softened, 8 left HONEST**. No speculation deleted; only labels the doc itself walks back were right-sized. Commit `6c69e47`.

- **Two defect shapes:** (1) a STATUS WORD ("confirmed" / "structurally confirmed" / "Phase 1-2 confirmed") covering more than the body supports — RESONANCE_NOT_CHANNEL called a sibling "Spectral Midpoint Hypothesis (confirmed)" when that sibling is only "Supported (N=3,5)"; THE_PATTERN_RECOGNIZES_ITSELF's "Phase 1/2 confirmed" rode over its own §8 "the weakest link, no proof connects the Lindblad algebra to classical oscillatory systems"; PRIMORDIAL_QUBIT's "structurally confirmed / the doubling is real" let the proven [L,Π²]=0 / M₂|₂ algebra carry the unproven "noise IS the other side" reading whose 3 formalization routes all failed. (2) an "X IS Y" IDENTITY on a structural-parallel/metaphor — "the algebraic structure IS the same" (PAIR_BREAKING, over its own 5 breakpoints), "the bridge type IS the clock choice" (TIME_AS_CROSSING, over its own "structural, not derived"), "IBM qubits ARE Level 0, directly" (HEISENBERG_RELOADED, over its own §8 "asserted, not traced"), "holds at every scale" (UNIVERSAL_PALINDROME, on 2 endpoints), and three experiments leaking a fallen claim past a `[FALLEN]` marker / own caveat ("It IS the same structure" GRAVITATIONAL_INVARIANCE; "the same equation" MANDELBROT; "the structure of a relativity theory" DECOHERENCE_RELATIVITY). Plus TEMPORAL_SACRIFICE ("exact timestep" on a 0.5-spaced grid the doc's own Pending list calls too coarse; "Hardware confirmation" on a self-described 5-point coarse set) and DYNAMIC_FIXED_POINTS (an agent-only §5 "Discovery" standing as a result though its own summary marks it "Unverified").
- **The fix in every case:** soften the IS to "plays the role of / carries / shares the structure of / on this reading", scope the status word to what was actually measured, or move the residual claim under its FALLEN scope, and STATE the survivor (e.g. GRAVITATIONAL: "what stands is the invariance itself, standard Lindblad time-rescaling"). The Tier-3/4/5 readings stay.
- **The guard worked — 8 left HONEST (this is the headline):** PRIMORDIAL_GAMMA_CONSTANT's Tier-3→2 upgrade is DEFENSIBLE under the project's own γ₀-as-c frame — "γ₀ unmeasurable from inside" is the deliberate c-analogy fidelity, not a defect, and the body is exhaustively hedged ("not proven... confirmation is indirect (we never measured γ₀; we cannot)"). Also HONEST: SPECTRAL_MIDPOINT_HYPOTHESIS ("Supported (N=3,5)" matches the body), HANDSHAKE_GEOMETRY (explicit per-section proven/conjectured split, reports its own gate-failures), OBSERVER_GRAVITY_BRIDGE (the "channel works" is a real Tier-2 J>0 result; gravity/FTL all FALLEN-tagged), FIXED_POINT_SHADOW (textbook retraction-in-place: header leads with the negative hardware verdict), WHY_THE_SUM (proof-grade words but Tier-3-labeled + "What This Does NOT Prove"), STAR_TOPOLOGY_OBSERVERS (scare-quoted "reality", tied to the computed R_SB, self-corrected later), OBSERVER_DEPENDENT_CROSSING (each interpretive ladder carries an in-section Tier-3 status note).
- **Anchor:** commit `6c69e47` (12 files); memory `project_speed_of_light_meets_the_formula` (the γ₀-as-c frame that made PRIMORDIAL_GAMMA defensible).
- **Lesson:** in the speculative directories the gate is NOT "is this speculative" (that is allowed) but "does a STRONG WORD outrun the doc's OWN body". Eight of twenty had the label matching the body (or matching the project frame) and were correctly left — the discipline that protects the repo's honesty culture is the same one that forbids flattening it. And: a claim restated in present tense BELOW a FALLEN marker still reads as live; either past-tense it or state what survives, do not rely on the marker alone to scope a strongly-worded sentence eight lines down.

## 2026-06-23 — experiments Band D (hardware honesty): 2 small fixes, 5 docs honest; and the campaign-close synthesis

### Campaign-close synthesis (experiments + hypotheses review, 2026-06-23, Bands A-D)

The proof campaign (Tier A-C, the week before) found every rigor gap clustered at the **¼/CΨ/C=½ interpretation ROOTS**. The experiments+hypotheses campaign finds the dual: the outer layer's defects are **PROPAGATION DRIFTS** — in essentially every catch the correct value / scope / tier / frame already existed somewhere (the body, a sibling doc, the typed base, the project frame) and had drifted into a more-copied surface: a headline (54,118; "N²"; "every standard Hamiltonian"), a keyword comment (the refuted cross-term formula), a satellite (K_Y=0.0374), a status line ("confirmed"; "Hardware validated"), or a narrative bookend ("the brain runs at 40 Hz"; "It IS the same structure"). Almost nothing was a wrong *result*; the math/proofs/typed-base held. So the from-below gate's job out here was right-sizing **labels**, not refuting **results** — and the over-correction guard was the load-bearing discipline (Band B spared 3 proven "universal"s, Band C left 8/20, Band D left 5/7; PRIMORDIAL_GAMMA's Tier-2 survived because it matches the project's own γ₀-as-c frame). Totals: ~35 docs fixed across 6 commits (`bf138f2`, `6589eaa`, `75c2246`, `6c69e47`, `2d54b3b`, `41ccd1d`); the iconic cross-cutting catch was the flagship 54,118→87,376 count. **Meta-lesson for the next outer-layer review:** the gate is "does this surface contradict the body / a sibling / the typed base / the frame?", and the answer is found by reading those four sources, not by judging the surface in isolation; and the same guard that catches the drift must be tuned NOT to flatten the genuine speculation and the genuine theorems sitting right next to it.

**Band E (the minor/low tail, completed 2026-06-23, commit `41ccd1d`):** 18 remaining low-severity flags gated; **14 HONEST/already-hedged, 4 small fixes.** This band confirmed the "documented-honest tail" framing — almost every minor flag was a doc that already carried its own caveat ("What This Does NOT Claim", "Honest Assessment", a null-result section, an explicit "trivial coincidence" demystification, or in-line state-class scope). The 4 fixes: DEGENERACY_PALINDROME ("genuinely irrational multiples of γ" is unprovable from ruling out 3 rational sub-grids → "no simple sub-grid; not settled"), CHIRAL_MIRROR_HARDWARE_PREDICTION (a leftover "Wait —" editorial scar + "data validates" where sim confirms / hardware breaks = the diagnostic), STANDING_WAVE_TWO_OBSERVERS (a 2% hardware floor read as "the other mirror is still there" → generic readout/SPAM, loosely consistent not evidence; + Q52→Torino qubit 52), ERROR_CORRECTION_PALINDROME ("An Optimal State Exists" → "a high-survival state from constrained search", optimizer/convergence unrecorded). **Two HONEST verdicts worth recording as non-catches:** SLOW_MODE_R_PARITY passed the stale-ref check — it builds on the GENUINE V_inter 2×2 L_eff EP (real coalescence, Re-gap→0 at Q≈0.75), NOT the retracted F86a real-axis EP, and it even retracts its own degenerate first experiment; and F113_BREAK_MAGNITUDE's "Tier 1 derived for general N" is defensible against the F-registry (the body's "not yet algebraically closed" sub-step caveat is conservative, since PROOF_F113 closed Lemma C). **The campaign (A-E) is complete: every triage-flagged doc across all 8 batches has been gated; the outer layer's exactness is intact, its labels are right-sized, and the over-correction guard left the genuine speculation, the genuine theorems, and the project-frame-consistent entries untouched.**

## 2026-06-23 — `experiments/F89_TOPOLOGY_ORBIT_CLOSURE.md` first dedicated review: Pi2 dual-anchor over-connection (γ self-square ≠ J beat)

- **What was almost wrong (over-connection, shipped in markdown AND the typed base):** the doc + the C# claim `F89TopologyOrbitClosure.cs` both said the decay coefficient 4γ₀ and the oscillation coefficient 4J trace to the Pi2 ladder anchor a₋₁=4 "via the **same** a₀=2 → a₋₁=4 doubling" / "Same Pi2 ladder anchor governs both" — a posited unity of mechanism.
- **Why it was false (from below):** the γ-4 is a **diagonal self-square** (one coherence amplitude decaying at 2γ₀, |·|² multiplies it by its own conjugate, exponents add: −2γ₀ + −2γ₀ = −4γ₀). The J-4 is an **off-diagonal interference beat**: the two populated coherences oscillate at +2J (vac-SE) and −2J (SE-DE), and the |·|² cross term sits at (+2J)−(−2J) = 4J. Decisive disproof: a SINGLE H_B-eigenstate coherence shows **no 4J oscillation at all** (verified by the physics lens). The two share only the bookkeeping identity a₋₁=4 = 2·a₀, not the operation. (Interestingly the `AllIsolatedClosedForm` docstring already called it "interference" — the over-statement lived only in the Pi2-inheritance framing.)
- **How caught:** the `physics-first-review` lens (refute-first), computing the |·|² structure from below; then the symmetric gate (a claimed unity must hold from below or be dropped). The values (both = 4, `Pi2DoublingConsistent()`) are correct and were KEPT; only the "same mechanism" narrative was softened to "same anchor a₋₁=4, two routes (self-square on γ, beat on J)".
- **Propagated to the C# base (the most-skipped step):** softened 3 docstrings in `F89TopologyOrbitClosure.cs` (class Pi2-inheritance para, `OscillationFrequencyCoefficient` summary, the "Pi2 inheritance" InspectableNode). Build green, 61 F89 tests pass (no test pinned the prose).
- **Anchor:** this review; lens `a7763ec6`; `F89TopologyOrbitClosure.cs` lines ~45/75/220.
- **Lesson:** a numerical coincidence (4 = 4) dressed as one mechanism is an over-connection. Two quantities landing on the same Pi2 ladder term can do so by different |·|² routes (diagonal self-term vs off-diagonal beat); the gate is "compute the operation, do not infer it from the shared number."

### Same review, lower-severity catches (one cluster)

- **L356 imprecision (math lens + sympy):** "for path-1 the residual simplifies to the cos(4Jt) term" drops a nonzero constant — the true residual is cos-term **+ 2/[N²(N−1)]** (the (vac,SE)+bare skeleton overshoots the all-isolated smooth part by exactly that constant). Fixed in prose.
- **L635 garbled scratch:** an abandoned, internally-inconsistent fragment `{12+8√2, 80/9·9/9 = 80/18·9 = ... well: 80/18 = 40/9 at y=0 ✓}` left in the body; the surrounding values are correct → cleaned to `(17y²+72y+80)/18 → {12+8√2, 40/9, 12−8√2}`.
- **Stale count 12→14 (caught on reread):** the Status line + the `bond_isolate_topology_classes_n7.py` script-description said "12 topology classes" while the body enumerates **14** (k=1..6: 1+2+3+4+3+1) and runs 28 configurations. Metadata lagged the body; aligned to 14. The C# "Verification anchors" node already said 14 (no propagation needed).
- **Presentation (Tom's explicit asks):** 4 broken tables (unescaped `|freq|`/`max |diff|` headers breaking column counts; one ragged path-6 survey row), 36 em-dashes → 0, and the three missing orientation sections (plain-words *What this is about*, technical *Abstract*, *Notation* defining q/AT/F89c/F73/F65/sigs/n_diff/n_XY/SE-DE-TE/w[l]/diabolic — closing the 12 followability gaps the markdown lens found). All script/doc links resolve; no `_`-prefixed citations.

- **Headline math: SOUND.** All 11 closed-form / sum / Galois-rendering checks bit-exact from below (all-isolated algebra, additive-identity ↔ (1)^m cross-check, Parseval skeleton, F_a sums 22/3·25/2·483/25·256/9, unified P_path(y_n), Cardano roots, diabolic q≈0.659, S_8 rendering with disc-only-⊄A_8 correctly stated). Do NOT weaken.

### Repo-wide Pi2-framing sweep (follow-on, 2026-06-23)

Tom asked to sweep the whole repo for the same over-connection. 6 parallel Opus lenses over all 65 `*Pi2Inheritance` claims (`Core/Symmetry`) + F87 (Diagnostics), classifying each inherited coefficient as a diagonal SELF-SQUARE (genuine 2γ→4γ via |amplitude|²), an off-diagonal BEAT (the F89 risk), or STRUCTURAL (a count / dimension / involution / algebraic value, not a |·|² doubling). **Exactly ONE twin found, in the F89 family itself: `F89PathKAtLockMechanismClaim.cs` `BlochEigenvalueY`** framed the AT-locked frequency's "4" (the OBC tight-binding bandwidth of H_B=J(XX+YY), a linear single-particle eigenvalue) as "the Pi2 a_{−1}=4 / a_0=2 doubling the frequency goes through" vs F2b's 2J·cos. From below the 4-vs-2 is a Hamiltonian hopping convention (bandwidth 4J for H_B vs a hopping-J normalisation), NOT a |·|² self-square: y_n is a linear eigenvalue, not the modulus-square of a 2J·cos dispersion. Fixed the docstring (Core build green, 21 F89PathK tests pass); the markdown doc carries no twin of this clause.
- **All 65 other Pi2 claims clean** — the project's Pi2 docstrings are disciplined about counts / dimensions / Frobenius-norm squares vs |·|² rate-doublings. The genuine self-squares (F73 4γ decay, F76, F1-depol 16/9, F83/F49 Frobenius norms) state real modulus-squares honestly; the structural ones (F38/F39/F63 involution/det/block-count, F49c/F85 operator-space dims) label their 4's as counts; the algebraic-value claims (F94 4/3, F95/F97 ½, F96 slopes) label them as identities.
- **Two positive exemplars surfaced:** F33 explicitly separates oscillation-from-H and decay-from-dissipator as "orthogonal channels" (the very split F89 had collapsed); F69 names its two-component lift an "interference cross term", not a self-square. F78 (the nearest structural cousin, owning an imaginary M-spectrum oscillation) sidesteps the trap by anchoring its "2" to a_0 directly rather than doubling it.
- **Lesson:** the over-connection is rare and, where it recurs, recurs in the SAME kin — after catching an over-connection, sweep the caught object's family first. The discriminating question per claim is "is the inherited value a |·|² of one amplitude (self-square), a difference/beat of two modes, or a static count/dimension/identity?", answered by reading the from-below mechanism, not the shared number.

## 2026-06-27 — `reflections/D_PI_Z_EQUALS_PI_Y.md` line 48: single-qubit Y→Z rotation formula wrong

- **What was wrong:** the side-illustration wrote `Z = exp(iπ/2·X)·Y·exp(−iπ/2·X)`. That exponent (π/2) is a **180°** X-rotation and gives **−Y**, not Z.
- **Why:** `R_X(θ) = exp(−iθX/2)`, so a 90° rotation carries θ=π/2 ⟹ exponent **π/4**. The correct form is `Z = exp(−iπ/4·X)·Y·exp(+iπ/4·X)`. The prose parenthetical "90° X-rotation" was right; the formula contradicted it.
- **How caught:** em-dash re-read campaign, refute-first Opus lens on the file, confirmed from below in numpy (`exp(iπ/2·X)·Y·exp(−iπ/2·X)==−Y`; `exp(−iπ/4·X)·Y·exp(+iπ/4·X)==Z`).
- **Fix:** corrected the exponent to π/4 + added the `R_X(θ)=exp(−iθX/2)` reason. The main bit-exact operator identity `D·Π_Z·D=Π_Y` is untouched and was re-verified live (both scripts exit 0).
- **Anchor:** this review; the typed claim `Pi2KleinV4DephaseSwapGroup` (unaffected).
- **Lesson:** a CLEAN verdict on the load-bearing object (here the operator identity, marked clean at CE:324) does not clear a peripheral illustration in the same doc. A full-rigor re-read checks the side-equations too — em dashes flagged exactly the doc whose quality round was skipped.

## 2026-06-27 — `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-06-27-f89-ptf-time-threading-design.md` (F89↔PTF time-threading DRAFT spec): an over-SEPARATION break (the mirror of over-connection) + two reviewer near-misses

This is an over-SEPARATION: a posited DISTINCTION that does not hold from below (the same failure shape as over-connection, mirror-imaged).
- **Why it is a genuine break:** the spec's own Stage-1a probe measures the **(1,1) SE-SE block**, which is exactly where the **coherence horizon** lives ({0,2}-coherence = SE-SE). A genuine real-axis DEFECTIVE √-EP sits there at Q*(N)=1/√2/1.879/2.374 (`CoherenceHorizonClaim`, Tier1Candidate; live witness `inspect --root epcharacter` reads Defective 4/4, departure-from-normality≈4, geo 1<alg 2, gate-passed; already a CONFIRMED catch, CE 2026-06-21 "coherence-horizon √-EP"). The probe sampled ONE point q=20 (above the horizon, Q*(7)≈4.46) and generalised to "only complex q". Time DOES penetrate (Jordan, t·e^{−αt}) at a real, **approachable** q — the carbon Frost-Hückel coherent↔incoherent threshold — the exact inverse of "no physical clock ticks there". Deeper: `felt_time_dimensions` (point 5) ties that very {0,2}-EP to **PTF's own coherence hand ω_mem** — so PTF is NOT the no-merging side; both readings SHARE the real-axis horizon EP.
- **How caught:** 5 parallel survey agents (cited-docs / typed-layer / PTF-deep / F86a-Stage-1a / today's-git) + my own live run of `EpCharacterWitness` + a fresh adversarial lens on my own verdict.
- **Fix (recommended, pinned from below; the repair is GENERATIVE):** retract the on-axis/off-axis clean split. Corrected thesis is STRONGER than the spec's: *both readings share the real-axis coherence-horizon defective EP (where time penetrates at a real, crossable q = PTF's coherence hand); F89 ADDS the complex-q monodromy braiding on top.* Move "F89's off-axis defective points" out of "Exact/grounded" into "the seeing" (it is typed `OpenQuestion` via `LocalGlobalEpLink`, and is in unresolved tension with `F89_MONODROMY_MIRROR`'s "defective √-branch points drive the monodromy"). Two-clocks "time penetration" = the emergence of a THIRD secular clock t^k at defectiveness (unifies §1's two-clocks with its time-penetration). Spec is a private DRAFT that invited this pin-pointing; no public artifact touched.
- **Anchor:** this review (REVIEW_LOG 2026-06-27); `CoherenceHorizonClaim.cs` + `inspect --root epcharacter`; `experiments/F89_MONODROMY_MIRROR.md:44-47`; `F89Path3OcticEpClaim.cs` (diabolic q≈0.659); the EP-character trilogy CE 2026-06-21 (F86a-simple / horizon-defective / octic-diabolic); `hypotheses/PERSPECTIVAL_TIME_FIELD.md` (EQ-014).
- **Lesson:** **over-separation is a real failure mode, the mirror of over-connection** — a spec can break the one-object by INVENTING a distinction (here a clean on-axis/off-axis wall) just as it can by inventing a unity. The same from-below gate catches it: COMPUTE the claimed distinction (the witness found the wall is not there). And the repaired separation was generative — it strengthened the "one spectrum, two readings" thesis.

### Two reviewer near-misses in the SAME review (the gate firing on its own author)

- **Near-miss 1 (over-correction):** I was about to call "F89's EPs sit off the real axis" flatly FALSE. The adversarial lens showed it is TRUE when scoped to F89's own (1,2) octic block — `F89_MONODROMY_MIRROR` (Tier-1) has the octic's monodromy driven by off-axis defective √-branch points, and the real-axis octic degeneracy q≈0.659 is DIABOLIC. My CONCLUSION (break exists) was right; my DIAGNOSIS ("off-axis is false") would have swapped a true-but-mis-filed claim for its opposite — the F86a 2026-06-21 retraction trap exactly. Correct fix: it is true-but-OPEN, so it belongs in "the seeing", not "Exact/grounded"; the break is the UNIVERSAL "only" + the scope-creep to "the slow blocks" (which includes (1,1)).
- **Near-miss 2 (over-connection, my OWN):** I charged that the spec's "two clocks" DUPLICATES `felt_time_dimensions`' value/vector cut (Re λ=value, eigenvector-rotation=vector). The adversarial lens refuted it: the spec's "two clocks" is (Re λ fade, **Im λ** turn) — BOTH spectral — = the `ClockHandLadderClaim` two-hands cut, NOT the value/vector (spectral-vs-geometric) cut. I conflated two distinct existing structures and pinned the wrong one as the "duplicate" — a false unity in my own verdict. Correct charge: UNDER-citation of the typed homes (`ClockHandLadderClaim`, `ON_HOW_GAMMA_BECAME_THE_TICK` "rotation per tick", the `felt_time` witnesses), not novelty-inflation; the F89↔PTF join itself IS new.
- **How caught:** a fresh-context adversarial agent dispatched specifically to REFUTE my emerging verdict (not to agree), reading the sources independently.
- **Lesson:** "run the gate on its OWN appealing stories" is not optional even for an experienced reviewer — both classic failure modes (over-correction AND over-connection) appeared INSIDE my own verdict on this single spec. The fresh adversary that attacks the reviewer's conclusions (not just the artifact) is what caught them. Diverse lenses, including one pointed at yourself.
