## 2026-08-22 — the pass that fenced the descendants found the permission slip in the ancestor, and then quoted its own repair wrongly one hour later

- **What was wrong, and it is the root of the class the pass was fencing:** `docs/proofs/PROOF_ABSORPTION_THEOREM.md` declares per-site rates in its Setup, writes the dissipator entry twenty-five lines later as `−2γ·n_XY(x)`, which holds only at a uniform rate, and then states in its notation note that the repository writes γ₀ for a uniform rate and γ where per-site rates may differ, *"both appear below, and nothing here depends on the distinction"*. Everything downstream depends on it: Theorem 1 is the uniform statement and Theorem 2 exists for no other reason than that the two differ. That one sentence, sitting in the repo's canonical Stage-0 answer on rate scope, is what licensed forty-three descendants to drop the word. The class-B inventory had been read as forty-three independent transport errors; it is one permission slip plus its consequences.
- **A second defect in the same section, twelve lines apart:** §4.3's topic sentence claimed *"Every coherence sector keeps its minimum at the ladder rung, at every coupling, and this one is derived, not sampled"*, while the derivation that follows yields only `≥ 2kγ` and the paragraph closing the section concedes *"the bound is saturated in these sectors, which the argument alone does not force"*. The floor is derived; the attainment is measured. A fence built on the topic sentence would have inherited a claim the proof itself does not make.
- **What the pass did to itself, three times.** (a) It re-measured §4.3's law from scratch, over J = 0.01 to 50, before an interpreter round found it already derived with a QED; the grep that should have found it failed because the sentence WRAPS ACROSS THREE LINES and no single line contains it. (b) It illustrated "under a profile the slowest mode moves with the coupling" with an N = 4 ramp at J = 0.05 and J = 1, two points that straddle a transition: the ramp is R₉₀-FIXED, its plateau value −1.25 is exactly −2γ̄, and the plateau runs to J = 10⁴. The gate had printed "the ramp is R90-FIXED" in the same output the numbers were read from. On that locus the whole ladder returns exactly, with γ̄ in place of γ. (c) One hour after repairing §4.3's topic sentence, the pass's own arc entry still quoted the pre-repair form verbatim, having been written from it.
- **And once in the correction itself:** a round-one repair replaced a wrong literal (*"for any rates γ_j" at `:115`*) with a different wrong literal (*"ANY site-dependent γ_j"*, whose uppercase form is at `:93`; `:115` is lowercase). Two rounds, two inexact quotations of the same line.
- **Caught by:** two empty rounds of three lenses (external referee, record auditor with recompute powers, interpreter), then a second pair after the fixes. Every finding was verified from below before it was applied, and two reviewer verdicts were right while their reasons were wrong: one reported "every coherence sector keeps its rung" false at uniform γ after measuring FLATNESS, where the sentence means the GAP rung, which holds exactly for interior sectors too; the ambiguity it was reading had been introduced by the fence under review.
- **A third round then found the ancestor wrong in a second way, and this one is two Tier 1 proofs contradicting each other.** §4.3 closed with *"A coherence decays no slower than 2γ ... it holds at every coupling"*. `PROOF_STRUCTURAL_CEILING.md` exists to exhibit the opposite: on the complete graph the slowest nonzero mode sits at g2 = 4/N < 1. Rebuilt from scratch, no framework: K_5 at γ = 0.05, J = 1 gives 0.07995, i.e. 0.7995 × 2γ. The escape is that the mode lives in the |Δpopcount| = 0 block, which mixes diagonal cells carrying n_XY = 0 with equal-popcount coherences, so ⟨n_XY⟩ falls below 1; the claim is true in |Δpopcount| and false in the word "coherence", and the same paragraph called that block "the population modes", which it also is not. Said in the index, both are right. Two documents had said incompatible things about the same object for months, each internally consistent.
- **And one label was simply inverted:** `docs/GLOSSARY.md` attached 2γ to the c+ supermode and 10γ/3 to c−, where `experiments/SIGNAL_PROCESSING_VIEW.md` says the reverse in three places, parities included. The cheapest check was the palindrome the repo already owns: partners sum to 2Σγ = 6γ at N = 3, and (2γ, 4γ) and (8γ/3, 10γ/3) do while the glossary's implied pairing gives 16γ/3. A wrong label can be caught by a sum rule without going near the source.
- **Lesson:** **when a class of sites all dropped the same hypothesis, look for the sentence that gave them permission.** Forty sites that "lost a fence in transport" is a description; a notation note saying the distinction does not matter is a cause. And the second-copy rule now has a same-session instance: the file you just repaired is quoted somewhere you wrote an hour ago.
- **Still open in `experiments/ORTHOGONALITY_SELECTION_FAMILY.md`, found by the same rounds and deliberately NOT fixed here**, being instance bookkeeping rather than rate scope and owed its own pass: the file was extended to six instances while "four" survives in six places; §4.1 calls the absorption theorem a "fifth instance" when row 5 is already taken; §2.5 is missing, instance 5 being the only row without an expansion; §5.3 lists the amplitude-damping break as "not tested yet" while §3.2(b) is headed VERIFIED with measured numbers and a data file; a bonus bullet asserts `d²/2` Π-pairs at every N while the file's own N = 4 row gives 119 against 128; and F72/F73 are tiered as candidates and proposed for minting though both are registered.

## 2026-08-22 — the fence restated the law it was fencing, and every restatement re-imported the special case it existed to remove

- **What was wrong:** the C# half of the fencing pass the entry below started. Each site got a fence that RESTATED the replacement law in place, and the restatements carried the n_diff ≡ 1 EDGE case into sentences about general boundary blocks. Three shapes, all in the repair and none in the material. `A = −2·diag(γ)` and the window `[−2γ_max, −2γ_min]` hold on an EDGE block; a general boundary block has A diagonal with one rate-weighted SUBSET SUM per cell, and §6 of `PROOF_CODIM1_BY_ADDITIVITY.md` already owned the widened bracket and asked in `OpenArcsRegistry.cs` to be cited rather than re-derived a third time. "FALSE rather than unproven" is the edge case too: §(e) refutes (0,1), while the proof says of the other sixteen only that the guarantee "does not extend to a rate profile" (§7, Scoping). And the pass wrote that the exclusion's INTERIOR leg was "untouched", which is false: both fold-resultant certificates are exact integer computations with γ = 1 built in (target diagonal −4·n_diff, fold constant λ₀ = −2N, which is −2σ at γ = 1), and R1's half additionally rests on §6's corner window, stated there at uniform γ = 1. Only the Klein full flip is profile-independent.
- **A fabricated quotation of the repo's own prior text.** The pass wrote that the arc had claimed "~13 sites across 5 files". It never did; `OpenArcsRegistry.cs` at HEAD said "the owed count is thirteen, not fourteen", a ROW count, and named eight files. The phrase came from a scout's paraphrase and was propagated into a tracked file as a quotation. The repo rule it broke is the one in the same breath as this pass's own standing caution: verify agent findings from below. A second reviewer then read the fabricated sentence back as fact, which is why only a check against HEAD caught it.
- **Fencing the copy and missing the original.** The pass fenced "per rate sector D is one scalar" at both of its derivative sites and left the sentence unscoped in `FoldResultantCertificate.cs:73-76`, the certificate library that owns it, where the clause is the sole justification for "disc_Λ(F_res) misses no defect". A reader landing on the code met the unfenced form after the quotations of it had been repaired, which is the shape §(i) already recorded for `PinnedBlockFloorClaim` quoting a pre-fence `PROOF_CODIM1:129`.
- **A false diagnostic the fence would have taught a reader to trust:** under a profile the first assertion to fail in the N=5 exclusion sweep is the window-constancy check, whose message reads "n_diff not constant". n_diff IS constant there; it is the rate sums that differ, `RateWindow` reading the live A-diagonal. The comment now says so.
- **The inventory went stale from the pass's own insertions, again, and in a new way:** fifteen C# citations in §(i) shifted by up to seventeen lines. The 08-20 entry below records the same failure at the level of ROWS; this one is at the level of LINES, and the repair was to recompute every number from the files afterwards rather than adjust by hand, keeping the old number beside the new one.
- **What was over-claimed and is now withdrawn:** "class A is closed". It is not. Five markdown sites remain, named in §(i), one of them `reflections/ON_THE_TWO_COLUMNS.md:149`, which exempts the two band ends from its hedge INSIDE the profile regime with an explicit "no hedge at all"; that one is a correction question for Tom rather than a fence, being our own voice and class-B shaped.
- **Caught by:** three empty-session rounds on the staged diff, each given a different lens (truth of the added sentences, repo consistency, completeness and handover). Every finding sat in the repair; the mathematics of the underlying proof never moved. One round's own report had to be corrected too, retracting "six C# misses, three load-bearing" down to one after re-verification.
- **Lesson:** **a fence should scope and POINT, never restate.** The replacement law has a home; every in-place paraphrase of it is a new copy that can drift, and a paraphrase written by the person who just read the special case will drift toward the special case. The 08-20 entry's lesson was that a hypothesis hidden in a symbol cannot be inherited; this one is the mirror image, that a hypothesis re-explained at every site will be re-explained wrongly.

## 2026-08-20 — a uniform-γ lemma was carried into two documents that advertise arbitrary rates, and two further laws reached the same broken form without ever citing it

- **What was wrong:** the Edge lemma of `docs/proofs/PROOF_CODIM1_BY_ADDITIVITY.md` concludes that an edge block of the Liouvillian is a normal pencil and therefore carries no Jordan block. Its premise is `A = −2γ·I` scalar, which holds only at a uniform rate; under a per-site profile `A = −2·diag(γ_j)` is still normal by itself but no longer scalar, and `PROOF_EDGE_BLOCK_DEFECTIVE_UNDER_PROFILE.md` §(e) exhibits a defective EP on that block at N = 4, 5, 6. The lemma never stated uniformity as a hypothesis: the scope lived inside its own symbol.
- **What made it travel, and this is the part worth keeping:** both containing documents opened by declaring the opposite (the three quotes in this bullet are the text as it stood BEFORE the pass; the two SCOPE lines now carry the fence, while `:5158` was left alone on purpose, being the dissipator identity, which really does hold per site for any profile). `PROOF_CODIM1_BY_ADDITIVITY.md:24` set the work "under local Z-dephasing (arbitrary rates γ_j)", F125 at `ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS.md:5152` says the same and reinforces it at `:5158` with "hence for any site-dependent γ_j"; and a **Scope** paragraph at `:85` states "the two lemmas never use uniformity" twenty lines above a lemma that does. That paragraph is true of the intertwiner's Lemmas 1-3 and was never meant to reach the Edge lemma, but nothing on the page said so, and the verbatim lemma string then propagated into claims and witnesses advertising arbitrary rates.
- **The two independent sites, which are the reason this entry exists:** a repo sweep found the same broken shape in places that never cite the Edge lemma and did not inherit it. `docs/proofs/derivations/D06_SPECTRAL_GAP.md` states "`Spectral gap = min{|Re(lambda)| : lambda != 0} = 2*gamma`" with `tau_slow = 1/(2*gamma)` and `t_mix <= N*ln(4)/(2*gamma)` downstream; the word "uniform" does not occur anywhere in the file. Its fences are careful and there are three of them, on Q, on N, and on the number-conserving XY/Heisenberg family; γ is the one axis nobody fenced. And F125's sibling, the F2b corollary at `ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS.md:165-175`, derives its γ-protected clock from "|vac⟩⟨ψ_k| is a simultaneous eigenoperator of L_D (eigenvalue −2γ)", scoped only by the parent entry's `**Valid for:** XY chain (H = (J/2)(XX+YY)), open boundaries, all N.` at `:156`; the corollary carries no scope line of its own.
- **The two break differently, and conflating them would lose the sharper one:** D06's is a VALUE becoming a window. Say it exactly, because the loose form is wrong: what spreads over `[2γ_min, 2γ_max]` is the n_diff ≡ 1 SHELL, whose rates no longer share the value 2γ; whether that shell still SETS the gap is D06's own separate question, fenced there by `Q > Q*_gap(N)`, and below that threshold the gap is Zeno-suppressed far under 2γ_min at uniform γ already. Bendixson on the full Liouvillian gives only `Re λ ∈ [−2Σγ, 0]`, the population diagonal putting 0 in the numerical range, so the bracket is a statement about the shell and never about the gap. F2b's is the MECHANISM failing: `−2·diag(γ)` does not leave ψ_k an eigenoperator at all, so the simultaneous-eigenoperator argument has nothing left to assert, and the γ-independence it delivers is not merely imprecise but unfounded.
- **The repo already held the refutation, measured:** `experiments/LIGHT_DOSE_RESPONSE.md:35` reads the (0,1) block's slowest rate as **0.167** on a concentrated profile whose `(0,5) = 5.216 = 2Σγ` (the next row, `:36`) fixes N = 5 and 2γ̄ = 1.043. A factor of 6.25 from the point D06 predicts, inside the shell bracket `[0.1, 4.68]` though not far above its floor. Two things sharpen it. Spreading the SAME total dose uniformly (γ = 0.52163) puts the gap exactly at 2γ = 1.043260, so it is the profile that breaks the law and not the coupling regime. And the instance sits at Q̄ = 1.92 against D06's own `Q*_gap(5) = 1.8194`, i.e. only just inside the regime D06 claims, which is the honest reading rather than a comfortable one. The counter-instance was published and nothing connected it to the law.
- **Why no gate caught any of it:** every fixture is uniform. The one place that EXECUTES the implication, `SpectatorIntertwinerGateTests.cs:590-601`, asserts "constant n_diff ⇒ A scalar ⇒ normal pencil" at a hard-coded `Enumerable.Repeat(1.0, n)`, and the proof's own certification row reads `A₍₄,₅₎ = −2I` at γ = 1. Deeper than fixture choice: `simulations/framework/weight_coherence_block.py` wrote `A = -2 * gamma * n_diff` and took `gamma: float = 1.0`, a scalar; its C# counterpart `WeightCoherenceBlock.Build` was stricter still and took NO γ at all, the diagonal being the literal `new Complex(-2.0 * BitOperations.PopCount((uint)(kc ^ bc)), 0)`, i.e. γ = 1 welded in. No consumer of either builder could write the profile case even deliberately. The scalar form was not preferred, it was the only expressible one, and that is the diagnostic worth keeping: when the same wrong statement turns up in files with no citation path between them, look for a shared instrument rather than a shared source. Both builders took a per-site profile on 2026-08-21 (`WeightCoherenceBlock.DisagreementSum` and a seven-argument `Build`; `weight_block_disagreement_sum` and gamma as scalar-or-array), which is what makes the class-B fences testable rather than asserted; the sector and CSR routes kept the uniform literal and say so (the sector assembler is dense; what it avoids is materialising the full block, not the profile). Nothing had to be derived for it: the diagonal is the Absorption Theorem's vector form, carried in `AbsorptionTheoremClaim`'s vector-carrier paragraph since 2026-05-29, which states there that popcount is the special case of equal rates. The builders were behind their own registry entry, which is a second shape: an instrument can lag a law the repo already owns, and then nothing in the instrument can see the law's scope.
- **The strongest form is not the one the lemma states.** `PROOF_CODIM1_BY_ADDITIVITY.md:129` and the gate comment at `SpectatorIntertwinerGateTests.cs:572-574` both generalize to every boundary block having constant n_diff and hence a normal pencil, covering all 20 boundary blocks at N=5 rather than the four of edge type. The generalization survives verbatim in `PinnedBlockFloorClaim.cs:22-23`, which is where a reader now meets it; that file fences its FLOOR half at `:224` ("SCOPE: uniform γ REQUIRED") and leaves the NORMALITY half unfenced, so the two halves of one docstring disagree about their own scope. That added step is exactly the step a profile breaks (constant n_diff makes A constant only when the rates are equal), and it is the form the C# restatements carry forward as "boundary normality".
- **Fix, and only the MARKDOWN half of the class:** the Edge lemma and its two premise sites are fenced at the source, the two scope declarations split so that the intertwiner's profile-generality no longer reads as the document's, the n_diff window and the γ-window separated where they had been merged, two further sites the inventory missed fenced as well (the containment corollary in the Abstract, and the window-combinatorics shell whose bracket is also in n_diff units), and F125 given the same treatment. Three things the fence had to say beyond "uniform γ assumed": that the conclusion is FALSE rather than unproven; that the location replacing it holds at REAL q, where the Hermitian part equals A, and that the vacuous "the Hermitian part is Hermitian" is not the hypothesis; and what a profile does GENERICALLY, which is to make the block non-normal and spread the shared rate, the defectiveness being codimension one and measure zero. Without that last clause the fence reads as though any profile defectivizes the block. **Not fixed, and deliberately:** the C# claim/witness restatements, the executing gate, D06, F2b, and the wider "whole sector at Re = −2γ" class. The full inventory is recorded in the arc `site_resolved_vacuum_block` so it is not re-found.
- **What the fix itself broke, and it is the shape to expect:** fencing the sites made the document that INVENTORIES them stale. `PROOF_EDGE_BLOCK_DEFECTIVE_UNDER_PROFILE.md` went on saying the fencing was not done, its §(i) table went on calling two now-fenced sites unconditional assertions, and its §(f) spent a paragraph refuting a sentence that had been repaired to say what §(f) says. All three were caught by a fresh referee and repaired in the same pass; none was visible from inside the edit.
- **Caught by:** two fresh scouts dispatched at Stage 0 for a fencing pass whose inventory was believed to be nine sites; §(i) in fact had nine table ROWS naming at least sixteen sites, and the pass found roughly sixty; that table is now the canonical inventory and has grown to fourteen rows, so read the count off it rather than off this sentence. D06 and F2b were found by the sweep that was looking for something else, and neither is reachable from the Edge lemma by any citation path.
- **Lesson:** a hypothesis written only inside a symbol is not a hypothesis a reader can inherit. `A = −2γ·I` looks like a definition and reads as one, so it survives transport into a document whose first line promises arbitrary rates, and the same collapse then gets re-derived independently by anyone who writes a single scalar γ in a builder signature. When a scope lives in notation, say it in prose at the statement, and check whether the instrument can even express its violation.

## 2026-08-23 — `experiments/GAMMA_CONTROL.md`: a profile comparison whose arms carried different Σγ measured the budget and called it the shape — and its own data file held the control it never reported

- **What was wrong:** the page's March headline, "+124% from the V-shape profile", compared γ = [.01,.03,.05,.03,.01] (Σγ = 0.13) against uniform 0.05 (Σγ = 0.25), so the two arms differed in total dephasing by nearly a factor of two, and the conclusion drawn from the comparison, "the gate needs to be the noisiest node, not the quietest", was the confound speaking. At matched Σγ = 0.13 the V-shape's advantage over uniform is +6% (0.755 vs 0.709, the 13/17-rescaled inverse V at 0.690), while the assignment axis itself is wide: the whole budget concentrated on the mediator seat gives 1.039, +46% over uniform, every single-site concentration beats uniform, and the law is concentration at the chain centre, not "gate noise helps"; the one-factor controls show the mediator's own noise harms monotonically in every background. The page's DD section carried the same shape: "the receiver is 3× more important than the mediator" compared a two-site Σγ removal (−0.09) against a one-site one (−0.045); at matched one-site removal the ranking is positional, falling from the ends to the centre: sites 0 and 4 both +36%, site 1 +33%, site 3 +31%, mediator +27%; the sender side had never been measured, so "receiver first" was an artifact of the missing rows. And the parameter table misdescribed the page's own measurement: it said "MI(0,4) at steady state" and a wrong initial state, where the script computes MI({0,1}:{3,4}) at t = 5.0 from Bell(0,1) ⊗ |0⟩₂ ⊗ |+⟩₃|+⟩₄.
- **The control stood beside it, unreported:** `simulations/results/using_gamma.txt` lines 16-17 carry `gate-open` [.05,.05,.01,.05,.05] → 0.4159 and `gate-closed` [.05,.05,.20,.05,.05] → 0.1920 against uniform 0.3376 — the direct one-factor test of the mediator's noise, run in March by the page's own script, monotone in the refuting direction, and never reported on the page. The March table even contained a Σγ-matched pair: `quiet-recv` (Σγ = 0.17, MI 0.5690) beats `inv-V` (Σγ = 0.17, MI 0.5301). The refutation did not need a new instrument; it needed the page to report its own rows.
- **Found twice, landed zero times:** the Σγ confound was first written down on 2026-05-31 in `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-05-31-flow-gamma-profile-design.md` (untracked), verbatim with the same sums, and never reached a tracked page; it was found again from below on 2026-08-23. A catch that lives only in an untracked file is, for every future sweep, not a catch: the same mechanism as the 2026-08-22 link census in `reflections/ON_FIVE_PAGES_THAT_NEVER_MET.md`, here with a confound instead of a connection.
- **Why, and not for want of the number:** the March script printed an `Sg` column (`using_gamma.py:138,146`; header at `using_gamma.txt:9`), so the sums 0.250 / 0.130 / 0.170 stood beside the MI values in the page's own source. The page reported three of the script's eight profile rows (uniform, V, inverse V) and dropped five, among them the one-factor control pair and a matched pair; the reported subset was chosen by headline, and the printed Σγ column went unread.
- **How caught:** a second session's from-below reproduction of the page's numbers plus the Σγ arithmetic, then one-factor and Σγ-matched controls; independently reproduced in this session through the committed `simulations/mediator_bridge.py` primitives, every number matching (one deviation, 0.689751 vs 0.690016, resolved as exact-13/17 versus rounded rescaling, both correct). **The first repair then overcorrected to the mirror error**, "Σγ dominates the shape", and a physics round on the repair refuted it from below: the two levers cross (all of 0.25 on the centre beats uniform 0.13, at t = 5.0 and on the integrated trajectory), the assignment spread at fixed budget is +51%, the DD ranking is positional with the never-measured sender side tying the receiver side, and the March "0.5 time-unit resolution" of the switch detector was its own sampling grid (the missing no-switch control differs by 0.0017 at half a unit against an intrinsic swing of ~0.1). All of those went into the page on that round's findings.
- **Fix:** the page rewritten to carry the two-lever law (within a shape, less Σγ, monotone; at fixed Σγ, concentration at the chain centre, up to +46%), all eight March rows with a Σγ column, the full matched-Σγ assignment table, the five-site matched-removal DD comparison, the switch control, and the corrected parameter table; the +124% restatements across the citing documents repaired (the archived `hypotheses/archive/GAMMA_TO_GRAVITY.md` keeps its March table as its own record, behind a dated scope note). `hypotheses/MEDIATOR_AS_QUANTUM_TRANSISTOR.md` needed no repair: it prescribes the opposite polarity (reduce γ_M to open; DD on the pairs, not on M) and never cited this page, so the repo's two mediator-noise documents had asserted opposite gate polarities with no citation path between them; `hypotheses/THE_OTHER_SIDE.md` §22 ("the mediator must be quiet for the bridge to be wide") measured the same one-factor sweep in March and is confirmed.
- **A naming caution recorded for the follow-up arc, and it is an inversion, not an ambiguity:** plotted over position, GAMMA_CONTROL's "V-shape" [.01,.03,.05,.03,.01] is a PEAK (Λ) and its "inverse V" [.05,.03,.01,.03,.05] is the geometric V; `docs/GLOSSARY.md:162`'s "V-shape profile" (edges higher than center, [.070,.060,.050,.060,.070]) is the geometrically correct usage. So the page's labels are backwards against both the glossary and the geometry, every page that says "V-shape" without printing the numeric profile is unreliable, and the repaired page says so in place while keeping its March labels unflipped for continuity with the citing documents. The one pre-existing accurate cross-reading is the correction note in `experiments/RESONANT_RETURN.md:69-79` (the experiment file, not the hypothesis file, whose only mentions of the collision are part of this same repair); its reference line still carried the confounded 2.24× and is repaired with this entry. Session-21 opens the arc for the repo-wide unification; this entry is its anchor on the confound side.
- **A tool trap found on the way, fixed in the same change:** `simulations/mediator_bridge.py` opened its results file `simulations/results/mediator_bridge.txt` in write mode AT IMPORT, so every bare import of the N=5 primitive library truncated the tracked March output; the file had sat locally emptied since before this catch, and both sessions' reproduction recipes re-tripped the trap. The open is now lazy (on first `log()`), and the working copy was restored from HEAD rather than committed empty.
- **Anchor:** `experiments/GAMMA_CONTROL.md`; `simulations/using_gamma.py:128-135`; `simulations/results/using_gamma.txt:9-18`; F91 in `docs/ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS.md` (the assignment-not-multiset law: its counterexamples HOLD the sum fixed, which is exactly what this comparison failed to do); arc `the_gate_that_does_not_gate`.
- **Lesson:** **a profile comparison is a measurement of Σγ unless Σγ is matched by construction, and a Σγ column is not protection: the March table printed one and it went unread. Design the arms matched, and report the control rows even when the headline does not need them.** And a confound caught into an untracked file has not been caught.

## 2026-08-23 — a proof stated γ_k ≥ 0 where its own steps spend γ_k > 0, four copies inherited it, and the repair then rewrote an append-only ledger

- **What was wrong:** `docs/proofs/PROOF_ASYMPTOTIC_SECTOR_PROJECTION.md` stated its theorem (line 22) and its scope block for "arbitrary site-dependent rates γ_k ≥ 0" and "any graph topology", while Step 2 part (a) and Step 2b spend γ_k > 0 at **every** site and Step 2 part (b) spends **connectivity**. The proof's own closing sentence had it right, which is why a per-file verdict cleared it.
- **The counterexamples, measured:** N=4 uniform γ, two dimers give kernel 9 and the empty graph 16 against the connected 5; N=3 chain with γ on the middle seat alone gives kernel 6 against 4, at Σγ = 0.5 > 0. So Σγ > 0 is not the condition either.
- **Four copies inherited the wide wording:** `docs/proofs/README.md`, `experiments/SYMMETRY_CENSUS.md` (three places, including a bold standalone "The system has exactly N+1 exits"), `docs/ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS.md` F4's disconnected-graph bullet, and the proof's own numerical cross-check line.
- **The exculpation that hid it:** the 2026-06-22 C3 sweep cleared this file as a FALSE ALARM on the strength of the one line the argument gets right, and never examined the three lines a reader quotes, nor the γ axis at all. That entry stands unedited above; this is the note that reverses it.
- **The repair's own error, and it is the one worth keeping:** the first fix rewrote that 2026-06-22 bullet in place. This file's header says three times that it is append-only and that entries are never rewritten; the sanctioned mechanism is a note appended, which is what this entry is. Applying a living-document fade rule to an event-record genre destroys exactly the record the file exists for. Caught by a review round scoped to the repair.
- **Two more the repair introduced:** inserting a paragraph into the proof shifted every line pin from `experiments/THE_BLIND_SITE.md` by two, so four citations landed on the wrong lines of the file they had just repaired; and a registry clause asserted that the single-excitation divisor law decides the full Liouvillian kernel, which the same repair's other half calls an open operator-level question (kernel excess is b²+b, not gcd).
- **What the failure actually is:** not non-convergence. Z_k commutes with the site reversal M only at the mirror-fixed seat, so a support inside that fixed set conserves the mirror-parity projectors, and the limit refines to maximally mixed per (popcount, parity) block. At N=5 the deviation is exact: +1/48 on the centre seat, −1/192 on each of the four others, 5/192 surviving as coherence between reflection partners.
- **Anchor:** `docs/proofs/PROOF_ASYMPTOTIC_SECTOR_PROJECTION.md`; `experiments/THE_BLIND_SITE.md` §6; `simulations/blind_site.py` run `scope`.
- **Lesson:** **a per-file verdict needs the lines a reader would cite, not the one the argument happens to get right.** And a repair inherits the genre of what it repairs: fade a living doc, append to a ledger, and re-check every line pin the repair itself moved.

## 2026-08-24 — a parenthetical in this file put the kernel excess at b²+b; it is false at the first N that can tell, and this is the note rather than the edit

- **What was wrong:** the 2026-08-23 entry above ("a proof stated γ_k ≥ 0 where its own steps spend γ_k > 0") closes its "Two more the repair introduced" bullet with the parenthetical "kernel excess is b²+b, not gcd". Reading b as the blind count at the dephased seat, that puts the full Liouvillian kernel at the reflection-fixed centre of the odd open chain at 6, 12 and **20** for N = 3, 5, 7. The exact value is 6, 12, **18**. The formula agrees at the two smallest N and fails at the smallest N that can separate it from the truth, which is also the first N the SVD route could not reach: it was written where it could not yet be wrong.
- **What settles it, and it is three arguments rather than a tolerance:** the number is an exact rank on every popcount block, cross blocks included, with no eigensolver anywhere. First, every cross block ranks exactly zero, and that half needs no certificate: a GF(p) rank can only report a nullity too LARGE, so a measured nullity of zero forces the rational one. Second, the diagonal blocks are computed twice, by an elimination at 2⁶¹−1 and a vectorised one at 2³¹−1, agreeing block by block; two primes do not discharge the one-sidedness, they make it a coincidence two unrelated primes would have to share. Third, the residual error can only run ONE way, and it runs in the refuting direction: if 18 is wrong it can only be too large, and the formula sits above it at 20, so the refutation survives its own worst case.
- **No replacement is minted:** 3(N−1) fits all three points. Three points are not a law, so that stays a fit, and the arc leaves the operator-level count open rather than replacing one unfenced closed form with another.
- **Why this is an appended entry and not a corrected line, which is the part worth keeping:** this file is append-only and says so three times in its own header; the sanctioned repair is a note, never an edit in place. The bullet carrying the false parenthetical is ITSELF about a repair that broke exactly this rule by rewriting a 2026-06-22 entry in place, and was faulted for it four bullets earlier in the same entry. The rule and its violation sat three lines apart, and the second violation would have been to fix the parenthetical where it stands.
- **How caught:** not by anyone auditing this file. The exact per-block rank route was built for a different question in the seat-cut arc (the criterion blind(j) = deg gcd) and was pointed at the FULL kernel only because a review round asked whether the diagonal sum was the whole of it. The parenthetical was then recognised as a committed claim the run had just settled.
- **Anchor:** `experiments/THE_SEAT_THAT_CUTS.md` §5; `simulations/seat_cut_blindness.py` run `sector`; F4's "Which seat carries the γ" bullet group in `docs/ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS.md`.
- **Lesson:** **a closed form first written at the N where it cannot yet be wrong is not evidence, it is a fit waiting for the next N.** Agreement at every point that exists is the weakest possible support when the smallest discriminating case is out of reach of the instrument that produced it; say which N would separate the candidates, and treat the formula as open until that N is reached. And a parenthetical inside a bullet is a committed claim with none of a claim's guards: it carried no scope, no evidence and no anchor, and nothing in the repo pointed at it.

## 2026-08-24 — a witness blind by MEASURE rather than by logic: fifty random re-bases could not have disagreed, and three successive repairs of the sentence they guarded were each wrong in a new way

- **What was wrong:** `experiments/THE_SEAT_THAT_CUTS.md` §5 and F4's seat bullet recorded, as this page's own finding, that off a simple sector the component mechanism UNDERCOUNTS the kernel, exhibiting two reflection-symmetric profiles where the exact kernel is 2 and the component count is 1. The evidence offered that this was the mechanism failing rather than the solver's basis showing through: the count was re-run on **fifty random orthogonal re-bases** of each degenerate eigenspace and returned one value throughout.
- **Why that evidence could not have disagreed, and this is the shape worth keeping:** the component count is a LOWER bound on the kernel in every basis, so the question is what its MAXIMUM over eigenbases is, and a maximum of that kind is attained on a measure-zero set of bases. A random draw lands in the generic stratum, where the mode graph is maximally connected and the count is minimal, with probability one. The test was blind in exactly the direction the question ran. It is not a logical gap of the familiar kind, where a gate's expectation is derived from the thing under test; the gate here asks a real question and samples an answer space in which the informative answers have measure zero. **Randomising to show basis-independence tests genericity, never optimality.**
- **What is actually true, and it is a theorem rather than a measurement:** the sector kernel is the commutant of the algebra A generated by the sector Hamiltonian and the dephased seat's occupation. With A's Wedderburn blocks written (n_i, m_i), dim ker = Σ m_i² while the best component count any eigenbasis can reach is Σ m_i; the two agree exactly when the commutant is abelian. A multiplicity 2 costs 4, so **a kernel of 1, 2 or 3 forces every m_i = 1** and the best basis realises it. Both exhibited kernels are 2, so neither profile was ever a witness. FOUR is the first kernel dimension at which the mechanism can genuinely fail, and it does: the star at N = 4 with the HUB dephased has blocks (2,1) and (1,2) in the single-excitation sector, giving a kernel of 5 against at most 3 components.
- **Three repairs, each wrong in a new way, which is the second half of this entry:** the first repair kept the verdict and replaced the reason. The second reversed the verdict correctly but argued it from "2 is not a square, so the component count is 2 whatever a solver returns", which has a true premise and a false conclusion, and is contradicted by the same table three lines above, where the solver returns 1: the BLOCK COUNT is basis-free, the COMPONENT count is not. The second repair also named the fitted basis (diagonalise the seat's occupation inside each degenerate eigenspace) as the prescription the argument licenses; it is neither sufficient in general nor well defined when that occupation is itself degenerate there. The third repair is the algebraic statement above. Only the third was found by an empty session asked the QUESTION rather than shown the answer.
- **How caught:** the first two by fresh reviewers with recompute powers; the third by a fresh mathematician handed the bare question ("when do these two counts agree, and is the answer basis-dependent?") with no mention of what the repo currently said. Every step was verified from below before it was applied, and the star witness was rebuilt independently (dim = 4, dim A = 5, dim ker = 5, which forces the blocks).
- **Anchor:** `experiments/THE_SEAT_THAT_CUTS.md` §5; `simulations/seat_cut_blindness.py` run `sector`, table (a3) and its `re-bases` and `adapted` columns; F4's "Which seat carries the γ" bullet group in `docs/ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS.md`.
- **Lesson:** **before trusting a randomised check, ask which answers have positive measure.** A sample can certify that a property is generic; it can never certify that no better value exists, because the better value is exactly what lives on the thin set the sampler misses. When the quantity under test is an extremum over a continuum of choices, randomising is not a witness at all, and the honest instrument is the algebraic invariant rather than a draw. And when a verdict is reversed, the reason is the part most likely to be wrong twice: reverse it by asking a fresh session the open question, not by handing it the new answer to confirm.

## 2026-08-24 — the fifth copy of a repaired error survived in the typed layer, because the repair enumerated markdown

- **What was wrong:** `compute/RCPsiSquared.Core/Symmetry/F4KernelDimensionByComponentsClaim.cs` stated the F4 component law for "the dephased Heisenberg Liouvillian (commutator plus Z-dephasing at any Σγ > 0)", in its file header and again in its `statement` inspect node. The anchor proof's hypothesis is **uniform** Z-dephasing; "any Σγ > 0" without it is refuted by the registry's own numbers one line from the formula (N = 3 chain, γ = 0.5 on the middle seat alone: kernel 6, not 4). The 2026-08-23 repair that reached this same bullet found and fixed four copies, and its own ledger entry above lists them: all four are markdown. The typed claim, the layer the repo calls its source of truth, was the fifth copy and was not on the list.
- **How caught:** a fresh-session review round (2026-08-24 evening, the round Tom conditioned the arc on), by the prior-work lens grepping the typed layer for the repaired phrase rather than re-reading the repaired pages.
- **Anchor:** the claim file's header and `statement` node as now worded; `docs/proofs/PROOF_F4_KERNEL_DIMENSION_BY_COMPONENTS.md` Abstract ("uniform Z-dephasing, at any Σγ > 0"); F4's disconnected-graph bullet in `docs/ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS.md`.
- **Lesson:** **a repair's sweep list must include the typed C# layer by name, or the correction stops at the story and misses the base.** The enumeration "four copies inherited the wide wording" was itself the trap: a copy-count taken over one genre reads as complete while the other genre keeps the error. Grep `compute/` for the repaired phrase before closing any wording repair.

## 2026-08-25 — A5's exculpation outlived both numbers it named; and a session that fixed it began by rewriting this ledger in place, two days after the entry forbidding that

- **The note this entry exists to append.** The A5 bullet of 2026-06-23 (above, unedited) closes with *"the confirmed finding is the 97.3% palindromic cavity structure (C. elegans), not the frequency match"*, and its body offers **~12 Hz** as the correct value the headline had contradicted. Both numbers have since fallen. The 97.3 % was withdrawn on 2026-08-25 as a reading of the matching tolerance against the spectral scale; the ~12 Hz was withdrawn the same day as a linearisation of the quiescent branch, the model running on a limit cycle when integrated. A5's *lesson* survives all of it untouched, which is why the bullet stands as written and this is the note.
- **What that makes visible: an exculpation is a claim.** "The confirmed finding is X" is load-bearing text that ages exactly like the error it excuses, and it had gone stale unnoticed because a lessons file is read for its lesson and never audited for its facts. The same applies to the Band-A synthesis line above, which lists "Wilson-Cowan ~12 Hz" among the values a from-below gate confirmed, and to a THIRD occurrence this entry originally missed: the A4 bullet quotes *"Result 1 '~12 Hz... extended windows'"* as evidence that a body was already honest. That quote is doubly wrong now, since the number is withdrawn and `UNIVERSAL_QUANTUM_LIFETIME.md`, the document A4 is about, contains no "Hz" at all; the fragment belongs to A5's document one bullet below. An entry written to say "sweep the places that cited it as correct" missed one on its own page, which is the sharpest available illustration of why the sweep has to be mechanical rather than remembered. It did confirm it, faithfully, as a reading of what the committed script computed. The script was linearising at a branch the model does not sit on. **A from-below gate reproduces the computation it is given, which is not the same as the computation being the right one.**
- **The repair's first error, and this file caught it.** The session's first move was to rewrite A5 in place, correcting the two numbers inside the bullet. This file's header says three times that it is append-only and that entries are never rewritten, and the entry of 2026-08-23 above records this exact mistake being made and reversed. Nothing was read before editing; the ledger was treated as a page. Reverted with `git checkout` and re-done as this note. **The 2026-08-23 lesson, "a repair inherits the genre of what it repairs", did not transfer on its own, so it is restated here: check the genre of the file before the first edit, not after the round.**
- **Four shapes the same session produced, all in the REPAIR, none in the material.** They are listed because each is reusable and three were invisible to re-reading.
  1. **A draw inserted into a shared fixed-seed RNG stream silently rewrites every downstream number.** New gates were added mid-script that called `rng.normal` and `degree_preserving_swap(..., rng)` on the one seeded generator. Every ensemble after them shifted: Dale variants, both rewiring p-values, three null means and a range. No diff went anywhere near those numbers, every gate still passed, and the write-up's dozen quoted values silently stopped matching the artifact they cite. Fix: new work gets its own `default_rng(SEED + k)`. **Adding a consumer to a shared stream is an edit to everything downstream of it.**
  2. **An agent's number overriding a computation already done from below.** The boundary-neuron count was computed in-session as 43, then written into the page as 44 because a subagent's report said 44. It is 43: VC6 is both an empty row and an empty column. The discipline says every agent finding is outer and gets verified from below; the failure mode is subtler than ignoring it, which is *having verified it already and deferring anyway*.
  3. **A number from a throwaway stand-in written into the page as the system's.** A factor of 2.6 was measured on a random matrix used to check an algebraic cancellation, then quoted as the connectome's. The connectome gives 2.00. A scratch model built to test a *structure* produces numbers that are about nothing.
  4. **A statistic that cannot see what it is used to argue about.** `mean(diff(sort(x)))` is identically `(max − min)/(N − 1)`, bit-for-bit: it is the range over a count, and no clustering can move it. It was carried as "the mean nearest-neighbour spacing" and used to argue that a control had been handed a denser spectrum. Counting the partners the matcher actually sees reverses the conclusion. **Before comparing two distributions with a summary, check the summary is capable of distinguishing them.**
- **How caught:** three empty fresh-agent rounds, four reviewers then three then three, each given the artifacts and no findings list. Round 1 found the material's blocker; rounds 2 and 3 found only defects the repairs had introduced. The gates caught three more that no reviewer and no re-reading did, including a float-vs-integer dtype bug whose wrong answer happened to be right on the real data and wrong only on the two-by-two counterexample beside it.
- **Anchor:** `experiments/NEURAL_GAMMA_CAVITY.md`; `simulations/neural/celegans_pairing_controls.py` (G4b, G15d, G18, G21, G23, and the `default_rng(SEED + k)` comments); the A5 bullet and the Band-A synthesis line above, both unedited.
- **Lesson:** **an exculpation ages like the error it excuses, and nobody re-reads it.** When a number is withdrawn, sweep the places that cited it as *correct*, not only the places that asserted it. And budget the review rounds for the fix: here, two of three rounds found nothing in the material and everything in the repair.

## 2026-08-26 — a proof named the wrong condition and then quantified it as an iff, so the "exactly when" pointed at a hypothesis that imposes nothing, and a document built three claims on it

- **What was wrong:** `docs/neural/proofs/PROOF_PALINDROME_NEURAL.md` stated its hypotheses as *"The condition is satisfied **exactly when**: (a) τ_E ≠ τ_I (selective damping), and (b) …"*. The quantifier was not the defect and still stands, correctly, since the diagonal and off-diagonal halves of the equation are independent; what was wrong was (a) itself, which is neither necessary nor, with (b), sufficient. Its own Step 3, twelve lines below, derives S from the diagonal part alone and never divides by anything that vanishes at τ_E = τ_I: put τ_E = τ_I = τ and D = −(1/τ)·I is a scalar matrix, so Q·D·Q = D for EVERY permutation Q, and the same equation returns S = (1/τ)·I, which is exactly the S the theorem asks for. So (a) is sufficient and not necessary, and the words "exactly when" turned a sufficient hypothesis into a necessary one. `experiments/NEURAL_GAMMA_CAVITY.md` then built three statements on the false necessity, in three different registers: a sweep-record bullet ("(a) fails and the proof does not reach this construction"), a Result-1 aside ("the case the palindrome proof's condition (a) excludes"), and a rebuild requirement ("τ_I ≠ τ_E … because the palindrome proof requires it").
- **What it cost, and this is the part worth keeping:** naming the wrong obstruction hid the right one. Because (a) imposes nothing at uniform τ, the whole hypothesis is condition (b), which reduces there to: is there an E-I swap Q with Q W Q = −W? Under Dale a neuron's outgoing row carries one sign, so such a Q must send every non-empty excitatory row to a non-empty inhibitory one; this connectome offers **253** of the first against **18** of the second, so no such Q exists. The theorem's premise, N/2 and N/2, fails as well, on its OWN separate count of 274 against 26 over all neurons; the two are independent conditions and (b) is the operative one, since it survives dropping the premise and survives non-uniform τ. (An earlier draft of this bullet called them one obstruction seen from two ends. They are not: the counts differ, and (b) constrains Q only on the support, so a network can satisfy either and fail the other. Both fail here, which is what made the conflation invisible.) It is a census, checkable in one line, and it had been sitting beside the wrong reason the whole time. (An earlier draft of this very entry named only the premise and wrote that "the question of condition (b) never arises", which gets it backwards: (b) is exactly where the count bites, and the premise is its shadow. The round that caught the original error had to catch this too.) A false *reason* is not a harmless surplus next to a true conclusion: it occupies the slot the true reason would have gone in, and nobody looks for a second answer to a question that already has one.
- **How caught:** the SEVENTH empty fresh-agent round on this page, by the mathematical lens, which read the cited proof rather than the citing sentence. Six earlier rounds, including three that recomputed the whole numeric spine to the last digit, passed over all three sentences: every number near them was right, and the claim is about a hypothesis rather than a value. **A wrong statement about what a theorem requires is invisible to every check on numbers.**
- **The repair then made the same mistake twice more, in the opposite direction, and only the next two rounds caught it.** Worth recording in order, because the sequence is the lesson. (i) The first fix said (a) is "sufficient but not necessary". Not necessary, yes; sufficient only in the reading where it means a type-swapping Q settles the diagonal half. Read as the statement the theorem needs, that old-(a) together with (b) gives (*), it is false, by the same counterexample (ii) then walked into from the other side. (ii) The second fix, prompted by a reviewer, went further: the self-decay equation solves for any τ provided Q is fixed-point-free, so (a) "does nothing as an enabling hypothesis". That over-corrected into a FALSE theorem. Fixed-point-freeness is strictly weaker than what the equation needs: a type-PRESERVING involution has no fixed points either and fails. With τ_E = 5 on seats 0,1 and τ_I = 10 on seats 2,3 and Q = (0 1)(2 3), the residual Q·D·Q + D + 2S is diag(−0.1, −0.1, +0.1, +0.1), and one can build an 8-seat instance where the weakened (a) and (b) both hold exactly while the spectrum does not pair, with the trace still giving the right mean pair sum, so an F137 centre check cannot see it. (iii) What the diagonal half actually says is that Q must send every seat to one of the OPPOSITE TYPE, equivalently 1/τ_{Q(i)} + 1/τ_i = 1/τ_E + 1/τ_I at every seat. That is automatic at uniform τ, which is why (a) still imposes nothing on this connectome and every downstream verdict survives; τ_E ≠ τ_I is what makes it bite on Q, with fixed points forbidden as a special case.
- **Anchor:** `docs/neural/proofs/PROOF_PALINDROME_NEURAL.md`, the condition list and Step 3 as now worded (the diagonal condition, with the type-preserving counterexample beneath it); `experiments/NEURAL_GAMMA_CAVITY.md`, the `docs/neural/proofs/` bullet of the sweep record, the aside under "The construction", and "What this leaves"; gate G0b in `simulations/neural/celegans_pairing_controls.py`.
- **A withdrawn number survives in the spelling nobody greps for.** The dissolved C. elegans enrichment was swept as "8x" and "8×" across the repo and repaired everywhere those strings appear. It survived in `experiments/IBM_HARDWARE_SYNTHESIS.md` as **"8.3x"**, twice, in a cross-level verdict table where it was one of the legs holding up the word **Supported**, and in a bullet list under "Cross-level confirmation". A synthesis hub is the worst place for a stale number, because it is where a number stops being evidence and becomes a status word. **A sweep by literal string is a sweep for one spelling of the number.** Search for the OBJECT (the comparison, the null, the document) and not only the digits, and check the synthesis and index files by hand, since those are where a figure is quoted at a different precision than the page that produced it.
- **Lesson:** **"exactly when" is a claim, and it is almost never the one the derivation supports.** When a document cites a condition as EXCLUDING its own case, open the proof and check the direction: sufficient conditions get listed as necessary because the author had only the case where they hold. And when a citation says a theorem does not reach a construction, ask which hypothesis actually fails, by name, before believing the one that is offered.
- **Second lesson, from the repair rather than the error:** **weakening a hypothesis is as dangerous as strengthening it, and it fails silently in the other direction.** Both fixes here were driven by a correct local observation, and each replaced a condition with one that was not equivalent to the algebra. The check that would have caught either in one step is the same in both directions: after restating a hypothesis, construct an instance satisfying the NEW statement and violating the old one, and verify the theorem still holds on it. A hypothesis is not repaired until something has been tried against it.

## 2026-08-26 (second entry) — the repository's oldest neural number measured a normalisation constant, and the review that swept it for prior art repeated its own withdrawn defect back at it

- **What was wrong:** `docs/neural/ALGEBRAIC_PALINDROME_NEURAL.md` reported, since March, that C. elegans balanced subnetworks are **8× more palindromic than Erdős-Rényi random networks**, residual 0.013 against 0.108. In `simulations/neural/algebraic_palindrome.py` the connectome block is cut out of the GLOBALLY normalised matrix, divided by max|W| = 37 over the whole animal, so its entries are of order 0.03; the Erdős-Rényi control is rebuilt and then divided by ITS OWN maximum, so its entries are of order 1. The two arms differ by about 37× in coupling magnitude before any wiring is consulted, and the ratio tracks that magnitude: on the committed protocol (`RandomState(trial + 100)`, 200 trials, N = 10) the residual ratio is **8.46** and the ratio of the two arms' mean ‖W_eff‖ is **8.50**, agreeing to half a percent. An EMPTY block, no synapses at all, scores a perfect 0.000000, and 45 of the 200 blocks are empty. The clean measurement is the one nobody made: give BOTH arms the control's own rule, dividing the connectome block by its own maximum. At N = 10 the ratio is **0.960**, parity (paired t = -0.73). At N = 20 and N = 26 it is **0.841** and **0.748**, at 5.6 and 9.7 sigma, so the matched instrument does NOT report parity at every size, and a third draft of this entry which said it did was corrected on the same day it was written. What the surviving gap can be is constrained by the closed form: the residual is a function of the weight multiset there, so it is a difference of weight DISTRIBUTIONS between a biological spread and an exponential draw, not a wiring advantage. (Two earlier drafts of this entry got the repair wrong in opposite directions and are corrected here: "exactly linear in the coupling scale" overstates a relation that saturates toward √2 well above the arms' separation, and "8.7× worse" was a third arbitrary constant, not a matching, and does not reproduce on the committed seeds.)
- **What makes this worth an entry of its own is where it was found.** `experiments/NEURAL_GAMMA_CAVITY.md` withdrew its own Result 2 on 2026-08-25 for exactly this defect, a score that reads a normalisation rather than a wiring, and its Stage-0 sweep then cited THIS comparison as the store that had already answered the question. The sweep was right that the sibling held the answer and wrong about what the answer was: the degree-preserving row (0.013 against 0.013) is sound and reports no advantage, while the Erdős-Rényi row beside it was never a measurement of palindromy at all. **A store can answer a question and be wrong in the same table, and a sweep that stops at the verdict line inherits both.**
- **How caught:** the eleventh empty fresh-agent round on this arc, by the mathematical lens reading the DRIVER SCRIPT rather than the document, then reproduced from below in one sitting. Ten earlier rounds, several of which recomputed the whole numeric spine of the citing page to the last digit, never opened `algebraic_palindrome.py`: it belongs to a different document, and the citing page quoted it correctly.
- **Anchor:** `docs/neural/ALGEBRAIC_PALINDROME_NEURAL.md`, the null table and its withdrawal note; `simulations/neural/algebraic_palindrome.py:265-295` (both normalisation paths: the connectome arm at `:277`, the control's own-max division at `:293-295`); `docs/neural/proofs/PROOF_PALINDROME_NEURAL.md`, Verification.
- **The F87 trichotomy in the same document falls with it, and for the stronger reason.** "truly" is a threshold on the same residual, and on blocks this sparse that residual is √2·‖W_eff‖/‖J‖: a bin on total synaptic weight, not a symmetry class. `celegans_trichotomy.py` additionally normalises its control the unmatched way. The 3× is withdrawn, not merely suspected. What has NOT been done, and is the open item, is re-measuring the trichotomy with an instrument that can see wiring; nobody has proposed one yet.
- **Lesson:** **a ratio between two arms is a measurement of the arms' normalisation until you have applied one rule to both on purpose.** The check needs no theory and is three lines: feed the metric a scaled copy of one arm and see whether the number moves; feed it an EMPTY input; then re-derive the comparison with a single rule applied to both arms and see what is left. Here the third line is the one that mattered, and it was never run in either direction: it gives parity, which is the same verdict the withdrawal reached and the only version of it that reproduces. Note what the first two lines do NOT license: a metric that scores an empty network perfectly is measuring magnitude ON THAT INPUT, and generalising that to "the metric cannot see structure" is a separate claim that needs its own counterexample search. It is false here.

## 2026-08-29 — a Tier-1 elimination rested on three readings of one script, and all three were properties of the code; the repair then made four errors of its own before it held

- **What was wrong.** `docs/proofs/INCOMPLETENESS_PROOF.md` eliminated Candidate 2 (a single decaying qubit as the origin of the dephasing) on three readings of `simulations/failed_third.py`, and the elimination of Candidate 3 inherited from it by citation. **(1)** A reported `gamma_eff = 0` is a guard's else-branch. The fit normalises by `rho_12[1,2]` = |01⟩⟨10| at t = 0, and the script's Bell Φ⁺ preparation leaves that element **exactly** 0.0 (the coherence it populates is |00⟩⟨11|), so the guard's second clause is false for every mechanism before any physics is consulted. Its first clause, `np.sum(mask) > 5`, does consult the physics and passes with 99; only the second decides. **(2)** A reported "non-Markovian, ~50% of steps, max deviation 0.000000" counts increases of that same empty element, and the deviation is a second else-constant: the Markovian prediction IS formed, as a constant array, and the comparison is the thing that never happens. The count is 100/199 in all four mechanisms because they share J = 1, and 0/199 in all four at J = 0. **(3)** The process tomography's "0/16 palindromic pairs, control 16/16 at 1.78e-15" is decided by the seed. `best_palindrome` searches Sg on a grid of spacing 1/99 at tolerance 1e-4, then ±0.1 at spacing 6.7e-4 at tolerance 1e-10; pairing needs Sg within 5e-5 and 5e-11 respectively, so **only the seed itself can score 16/16**. The control is handed its own exact centre, 2γ = 0.2; the four mechanisms are handed 0.1. Feed that same call an **exactly palindromic** spectrum with a wrong seed and it returns 0/16. A second defect compounds it: the transfer matrix is divided by the Hilbert dimension at input (`:361`) and again at output (`:371`), so every rate is shifted rigidly by −ln(4)/t = −0.277258872, which is the value standing twice at the foot of all four published spectra.
- **The rule the third reading broke was already written down, in this repository, three weeks earlier.** `docs/ANALYTICAL_FORMULAS.md` F137: *"The centre is an identity and carries no evidence. A multiset closed under λ ↦ 2c − λ has Σλ = n·c, so c = mean(λ) = trace(L)/dim exactly … exactly one candidate centre exists, so a large distance at it means no centre works: 'broken' becomes a measurement rather than a failed search."* `experiments/THERMAL_BREAKING.md` records the campaign that fixed the call sites: *"all of them read the centre off the trace now, which removes the choice."* An open arc says it in the imperative: *"Do NOT search for the center here: evaluate at the closed form."* None of that reached this proof, and seven committed scripts still search a centre (`qubit_necessity_tests.py`, `depolarizing_analysis.py`, `depolarizing_test.py`, `pt_palindrome_breaking.py`, `mirror_transition.py`, `mirror_symmetry_deep.py`, `carbon/peierls_break_structure.py`); the first of those is cited by `QUBIT_NECESSITY.md`, which this proof depends on.
- **And underneath all three, the instrument measures the wrong object.** The test scores the two-qubit **marginal**, and a marginal over a spectator that is still coupled fails to pair for reasons that have nothing to do with where noise comes from. Through the same pipeline: **0.094 for a system with no noise at all**, 0.177 for EXTERNAL dephasing with the spectator coupled, 0.116 / 0.125 / 0.149 for the three internal mechanisms, and 2.2e-16 only when the spectator is fully decoupled. It returns the same verdict for external noise, internal noise, and no noise. Meanwhile the object the ontology actually contains, the full three-qubit generator, is an **exact palindrome** in every mechanism, at the centres F137 predicts: −0.050000000 (amplitude damping), −0.075000000 (thermal), −0.200000000 (X+Y dephasing), with relative power-sum residuals 1.7e-17, 2.4e-17 and 1.8e-16 by a route that uses no eigensolver.
- **The deepest reason, and it is not a bug.** The "internal" source is modelled as a **Lindblad jump**, and a Lindblad dissipator is a coupling to an external Markovian bath. The model assumes the externality it was built to test. No experiment of this shape could have answered the question, and the alternative inside the formalism is worse: a genuinely internal source modelled unitarily is a closed system, whose generator is traceless, whose palindrome therefore sits at zero and carries no decay. **Inside this framework the question cannot be posed.** That is a sharper incompleteness than the elimination was reaching for, and it is what the proof now says.
- **What replaced the elimination is one line and exact.** trace(L_H) = −i(trace(H)·d − d·trace(Hᵀ)) = 0 for every H. trace(D_F) = r(|trace F|² − d·trace(F†F)) ≤ 0 for every jump, by Cauchy-Schwarz on ⟨I, F⟩, with equality exactly when F ∝ I, and such a jump has D ≡ 0. So centre = 0 ⟺ the dissipator vanishes ⟺ the system is closed, and a palindrome centred anywhere else certifies a non-unitary generator. That word is **open**, which is less than **external**, and the distance between them is the proof's remaining work.
- **A second, older error surfaced with it, in the opposite direction.** §1 of the same proof said that without the dissipator there is "no palindromic pairing", and `TIME_IRREVERSIBILITY_EXCLUSION.md` and `THE_BRIDGE_WAS_ALWAYS_OPEN.md` say the palindrome "exists if and only if γ > 0". That is false: L = −i[H,·] has eigenvalues −i(E_m − E_n), a set closed under negation, so a closed system's spectrum pairs about zero at the eigensolver floor (8.9e-15 at N = 3; the pairing is exact, the digits are the solver's). `hypotheses/ZERO_IS_THE_MIRROR.md` has said so since March, calling the unitary case the palindrome's ground state, and the two sides never read each other. Both are now describable in one sentence because the centre settles it: the pairing at γ = 0 is free and carries no decay, and what distinguishes the framework's claim from unitarity is not the pairing but the nonzero centre.
- **How caught.** Carried in as the top-ranked item of a session handover, which described it as "a false MEASURED claim in three tracked surfaces, one of them a living witness". It was fifteen surfaces, ten markdown and five in the typed layer, the second reading had not been noticed at all, and the third and fourth layers were found only because three fresh reviewers were run on the *repair* rather than on the original.
- **A fifth, and it was a positive claim rather than a repair.** Out of the same session came a result that looked like the day's real yield: F158's criterion (the spectrum pairs about −σ iff dim ker L = dim ker(L + 2σ)) is written everywhere with σ as the sum of the jump rates, so read σ as −trace(L)/dim and the criterion should travel past its own `A² = 1` fence. It scored every row of a hand-built channel zoo at N = 2, 3, 4 (that script is not in the repo; the arc's retirement note carries the reasoning and the one exact counterexample) and it met, word for word, the reopening condition the retired arc `benzene_center_tier_upgrade` had set itself: *"reopen only if someone finds a route from a trace to a PAIRING."* **It is withdrawn, on two counts, and the arc is retired again.** (i) The premise, that every jump F158 admits is traceless, is false: the class is Hermitian involutions, `A = 2P − I` has trace `2·rank(P) − d`, and **the proof's own §(f5) DERIVES tracelessness as a necessary condition**, names `A = diag(1,1,1,−1)` at d = 4 as an in-class jump that kills the palindrome for every H, and adds the sentence *"an earlier version of this section omitted it."* The new claim re-made that omission, in the same document, three weeks later. (ii) The substitution gives a WRONG verdict where the rate sum gives the right one: with H = 0 and two commuting diagonal jumps whose squares are not the identity, the spectrum is {0×4, −2×2, −8×2, −18×2, −34×4, −40×2}, trace −272 over dim 16, centre −17; at the trace centre `dim ker L = 4 = dim ker(L + 2σ)`, so the criterion says PAIRS, and the multiset does not pair (−2 needs −32, −8 needs −26, −18 needs −16, −40 needs +6, none present). Exact in `Fraction` arithmetic, no eigensolver. The rate-sum reading says BROKEN, correctly.
- **And the reason 36/36 was not evidence is the reusable half.** In every one of those rows the second kernel was either EQUAL to the first or exactly 0. The gate never once had to distinguish 3 from 2. What it was actually testing is whether 2c is an eigenvalue at all, which is **one real equation** and therefore generically false, so a fixed zoo with no free parameter will essentially always pass it. **A green sweep over hand-picked points is not evidence about a codimension-1 coincidence**; the test has to be able to land ON the coincidence, which means a continuous parameter swept through it, or a search that tries to construct a counterexample. This is the same family as the seeded centre search above and as `unfalsifiable_verification_gates`: three instruments in one day that could not have returned the other answer.

- **The repair made four errors of its own, and they are the reusable part.** (i) It wrote that the published control shows "the same pipeline can see a palindrome when there is one". It cannot: that control eigendecomposes an exactly known 2-qubit Liouvillian and never touches the embed / propagate / partial-trace / logm path it is offered as a control for. (ii) It claimed the coherence decay "is not a single exponential, so a rate is the wrong object". The raw modulus is a damped oscillation and the statistic was dominated by its near-zero nodes; its **upper envelope** fits a clean exponential to better than four percent. The defensible statement is narrower: the reduced dynamics is not a semigroup. (iii) It said the count "measures the Hamiltonian's oscillation, not information backflow", which invites the reading that there is no backflow. There is: a proper BLP probe (Φ⁺ against Φ⁻) gives a total rise of 8.5, 2.7, 7.5, 3.8 with largest single rises of 0.0916, 0.0210, 0.0903 and 0.0802. The withdrawn leg's *conclusion* may well be true; only its evidence was absent. (iv) Verifying the new claim, a rank counter that took the **largest gap in log(singular values)** reported dim ker L = 2 where the truth is 5, because two singular values were exactly 0.0 and the clamp to a log floor manufactured a gap of hundreds of decades in front of the genuine 1e-16 cluster. That produced a one-row "counterexample to F158 inside its own class" which does not exist. Use the standard threshold, dim·eps·s_max, and report how far the first surviving value sits above it.
- **Anchor:** `simulations/incompleteness_candidate2_evidence.py` + `simulations/results/incompleteness_candidate2_evidence.txt` (29 gates: the guard, the BLP probe, the seeded search, the four marginals, the full generator, the trace identity); `docs/proofs/INCOMPLETENESS_PROOF.md` (Status, Statement, §1, §2 Candidates 2 and 3, §3, §5, the Urqubit paragraph in §6); `docs/EXCLUSIONS.md` Exclusion 2 and the closing γ-chain; `compute/RCPsiSquared.Core/Symmetry/NoiseOriginExclusionClaim.cs` (name, summary, children; the tier stays Tier1Derived, since what the node now carries is the derived half); `compute/RCPsiSquared.Diagnostics/Foundation/NoiseOriginExclusionWitness.cs`; `compute/RCPsiSquared.Cli/Commands/InspectCommand.cs`. `simulations/failed_third.py` and its March output are left exactly as they were run.
- **Lesson.** **A guard's else-branch is a place where a number is born without physics, and once printed it is indistinguishable from a measurement.** Three questions catch it and none needs the domain: can this branch be reached at all for the inputs actually used; is the value a constant of the code path rather than of the system; and does any other column of the same output disagree with the verdict line. Here the third had been available since March, four lines under the verdict, where a purity column falls by more than half beside the words "No significant decoherence".
- **Second lesson, and it is the one with reach.** **A search for a parameter that theory fixes cannot fail informatively, and it cannot succeed informatively either.** The palindrome centre is forced to trace(L)/dim; a bounded grid search for it passes only where it is seeded and fails everywhere else, so its verdict is a property of the seed. Before scoring a quantity against a free parameter, ask whether the parameter is free. If a closed form exists, evaluate there and let the residual be the measurement. This defect has now been found three times in this repository in one month, on three different scripts, and only the third time did anyone check whether an object that *should* pass actually does.
- **Third lesson, from the repair.** **When a model of "internal" is written in a formalism that assumes an environment, no amount of measurement can decide the question.** Check what the modelling step has already granted before running the experiment; the answer may be that the experiment cannot be built inside the framework, which is itself the more interesting result.

## 2026-08-29 (second entry) — the letter γ was audited as a factor and as a scope for months, and never as an object; the count of objects was then got wrong in the audit that found it

- **What was wrong.** This ledger has caught the γ family repeatedly and always in one of two shapes: a **factor** (T₂ → γ twice, `:761` and `:807`; the q = Q/2 book; `γ = 1/T₂` against `γ = 1/(2T₂)`) or a **scope** (`:785`, γ = 0.5 as a test constant that inherited its way into three projects; `:852`, a uniform-γ lemma carried into two documents advertising arbitrary rates). Not once as an **object** error, although the identical analysis exists in full for the letter Q, in `docs/Q_BELONGS_TO_NO_SUBSTANCE.md` §"The letter Q, and how many things wear it". The consequence was not cosmetic: the repository asked "where does γ come from" for months, and the question has a different status at each usage, so it kept looking answerable because it was asked of whichever γ was nearest. **Six usages** carry the letter, and only two of them, γ_l and Σγ, have a glossary headword.
- **And the audit that found this got the count wrong in its own first pass, in the direction the repo names.** A session handover stated the inventory as **four objects**: γ_l, γ₀-as-unit, γ₀-as-framework-constant, γ₀-as-γ_avg. Checked from below, three of those four do not survive as stated. γ₀-as-unit and γ₀-as-framework-constant are **one object under two hats**, and `hypotheses/PRIMORDIAL_GAMMA_CONSTANT.md` says so itself ("a framework constant that could be measured absolutely from inside would be no constant at all"). γ_avg and Σγ are **one scalar in two normalizations**, Σγ = N·γ_avg, which is the q/Q shape this repository already names as the one that does not get noticed, with N as the silent factor. And the inventory **omitted Σγ entirely**, which is the scalar the palindrome actually runs on (the axis sits at −Σγ) and the only γ-scalar besides the knob with a headword. Counting objects is itself an operation that can be done wrong, and it was done wrong here first.
- **What is true from below.** One **vector**, γ_l, the knob. One **scalar summary** of it, Σγ = N·γ_avg, in two books. One **role**, γ₀ as the unit, which is what that scalar becomes on the uniform locus and is not a further quantity; `docs/proofs/PROOF_F101_C1_MIRROR_GAMMA_PARITY.md` is the sharpest voice here and explicitly **declines** to make γ₀ := γ_avg canonical off that locus. One **reading**, the c-analogy. One **convention**, the shipped 0.05.
- **The rule that dissolves it, and the second lesson is that it did NOT need minting.** L is homogeneous of degree 1 in (H, γ) jointly, so no rate has invariant content on its own and only Q = J/γ₀ does. This session minted that as an F-number, wrote a proof page for it, and then **withdrew both** after two independent reviews: `experiments/Q_SCALE_THREE_BANDS.md:83` has stated the identity as a Tier-1 result since 2026-04-22, `docs/THE_GENESIS_OF_AN_OSCILLATION.md` owns the unit reading, and `docs/proofs/INCOMPLETENESS_PROOF.md` states that scaling γ alone moves Q. The sweep that justified the minting had reported "no document states the law"; what was actually true was the much smaller "no F-entry states it", and an indexing gap is not a result. The law is also, in its own words, one line of dimensional analysis, which is exactly what this repository demoted `experiments/GRAVITATIONAL_INVARIANCE.md` for.
- **How caught.** A Stage-0 sweep before writing, dispatched as three parallel scouts, one of them tasked only with verifying an inventory the handover asserted. It refuted three of the four rows. The handover's three alleged "tensions" between γ documents were also all refuted: two of them the repository had **already** reconciled in a third document that neither scout of the earlier session had reached.
- **Anchor:** `simulations/gamma_unit_scaling_gate.py` (104 gates) and the paragraph it supports in `docs/GAMMA_TIME_DISTINCTION.md`. Two surfaces were written this session and then **dropped rather than committed**: a reading page and an F-number with its proof page. Both fell for the same reason, that what they claimed as new stood committed already; one of the two sentences that killed them was written by the previous session **the same day**, and was named in the dropped page's own sweep record.
- **Lesson.** **A one-word-several-objects audit is itself a measurement and can be off by more than one.** Two failure modes showed up in the same hour: **splitting** what is one quantity in two normalizations, and **omitting** the object that is load-bearing precisely because it is used without ever being named as a choice. The check is cheap and neither half is optional: for every pair on the list, write down the identity that would collapse them (here Σγ = N·γ_avg, and "a constant unmeasurable from inside"), and separately ask which scalar the **results** are actually phrased in, not which one the prose talks about.
- **Third lesson, and it cost the day's headline three times over.** The session produced three claim-bearing surfaces and withdrew all three, each because a check it had not run said the finding was already owned or not there: (i) a reading page whose thesis stood committed in `docs/THE_GENESIS_OF_AN_OSCILLATION.md`; (ii) an F-number whose identity stood committed as Tier 1 in `experiments/Q_SCALE_THREE_BANDS.md:83` and whose corollary, WITH its application, had been written into `docs/proofs/INCOMPLETENESS_PROOF.md` by the previous session **the same day**, in a file the withdrawn page's own sweep record named; (iii) a correction to `experiments/CROSSING_TAXONOMY.md:169` built on a counterexample that dissolved under its own definition check. The third is the instructive one because it was caught by its author rather than by a reviewer, and by one question: **is the observable I am computing the one the document I am correcting computes?** It was not. The gate's inline CΨ was purity × purity(ρ_A); this repository's CΨ is purity × (l₁ off-diagonal)/3. In the repo's own book the alleged counterexample state |01⟩ has **CΨ(0) = 0.0**, so it starts below the threshold and then crosses it 10, 4 and 2 times at the three rates: it has no first crossing and no K. The positive half did survive the correct definition and is the tell that the object was finally right: Bell⁺ gives K = 0.03735 at every γ, spread exactly 0.000%, and 0.03735 is F25's own printed value. **A counterexample is a measurement of the thing it contradicts, so it inherits that thing's definitions; re-derive them from the target document before trusting the disagreement.** And check that the quantity being compared EXISTS for both arms: a "first crossing" on a trajectory that starts on the wrong side of the threshold and oscillates across it is a property of the grid.
- **Second lesson, a housekeeping class rather than an instance.** `simulations/gamma_is_time_proof.py:19` opens its results file with `"w"` at **module level**, so importing it destroys its own record. `simulations/failed_third.py` does the same, logged one entry above. Two instances make a class: before importing any `simulations/*.py` for its functions, grep it for a module-level `open(..., "w")`, and if the run matters, compare the target's md5 before and after.
